########## FILE: app.py ##########
"""Quant Astro 本地排盘：印度占星 + 西方占星，共用一套人物档案。

启动：python app.py，浏览器打开 http://127.0.0.1:5300/（端口以 user_data/settings.json 的 server.port 为准）。

目录：
  common/          共用区：天文基础（星历、宫位、日出日落）、人物档案（前后端）、设置、页面外壳
  systems/vedic/   印度占星（KP、Vimshottari Dasha、南北印度盘）
  systems/western/ 西方占星（小行星、恒星、Lot、相位、圆形星盘）
  user_data/       设置和档案库（不属于代码，备份时连同它一起备份）

systems/ 下的体系是自动发现的：删掉某个体系的文件夹，程序照常运行，只是少了那个体系。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from flask import Flask

from common.paths import COMMON_DIR, SETTINGS_PATH, USER_DATA_DIR
from common.profiles import ProfileLibraries, create_blueprint
from common.settings import SHARED_DEFAULTS, SettingsStore
from common.shell import init_shell
from common.system import discover_systems


def create_app():
    USER_DATA_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    app = Flask(
        __name__,
        template_folder=str(COMMON_DIR / "templates"),
        static_folder=str(COMMON_DIR / "static"),
        static_url_path="/static",
    )
    app.config["JSON_AS_ASCII"] = False
    # Flask 默认会把返回的 JSON 的键按字母序重排：行星、宫头的顺序就全乱了。关掉后行序 = 后端的计算顺序。
    app.json.sort_keys = False

    systems = discover_systems()
    store = SettingsStore(SETTINGS_PATH)
    for system in systems:
        store.register_system(system.id, system.settings_defaults, system.normalize_settings)
    store.load()

    # 档案库：每套档案一个 SQLite 文件（user_data/libraries/），两个体系共用。
    libraries = ProfileLibraries(USER_DATA_DIR)
    libraries.select((store.data.get("session") or {}).get("library") or "")

    app.register_blueprint(create_blueprint(libraries, store))
    app.register_blueprint(init_shell(store, libraries, systems))
    for system in systems:
        system.attach(store, libraries)
        app.register_blueprint(system.blueprint, url_prefix=f"/{system.id}")

    app.extensions["astro"] = {"store": store, "libraries": libraries, "systems": systems}
    return app


app = create_app()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    server = app.extensions["astro"]["store"].data.get("server", {})
    app.run(
        host=str(server.get("host", SHARED_DEFAULTS["server"]["host"])),
        port=int(server.get("port", SHARED_DEFAULTS["server"]["port"])),
        debug=False,
        use_reloader=False,
        threaded=True,
    )

########## FILE: common\paths.py ##########
"""项目里用到的几个目录。"""

from pathlib import Path

BASE_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
COMMON_DIR = BASE_DIR / "common"
SYSTEMS_DIR = BASE_DIR / "systems"
USER_DATA_DIR = BASE_DIR / "user_data"
SETTINGS_PATH = USER_DATA_DIR / "settings.json"


########## FILE: common\settings.py ##########
"""settings.json 的读写。

文件结构：
    {
      "server":           {"host", "port"}                       共用
      "default_location": {...}                                  共用：过境盘的默认地点
      "birth_defaults":   {"calendar"}                           共用
      "session":          {"library", "last_profile", "last_system"}   共用：上次用的档案库、人物、体系
      "appearance":       {"coord_format"}                       共用：经纬度格式
      "layout":           {"tab_order": {体系: [标签页]}}         共用：各体系结果区标签页的顺序
      "systems": {
        "vedic":   {...}   印度占星自己的设置（由 systems/vedic 定义和校验）
        "western": {...}   西方占星自己的设置（由 systems/western 定义和校验）
      }
    }

各体系在启动时用 register_system() 登记自己的默认值和整理函数；
网页上改了设置时，用 write() 只改其中一节，写之前会重新读一遍磁盘上的文件，
所以程序运行期间手工改过的其它设置不会被覆盖。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import re
import threading
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Callable, Mapping

SHARED_DEFAULTS = {
    "server": {
        "host": "127.0.0.1",
        "port": 5300,
    },
    "default_location": {
        "latitude": 23.0331389,
        "longitude": 113.1010556,
        "timezone": "+08:00",
        "label": "默认地点",
    },
    "birth_defaults": {
        "calendar": "g",
    },
    # 会话：上一次使用的档案库、每个档案库里上一次选中的人物、上一次打开的占星体系。
    "session": {"library": None, "last_profile": {}, "last_system": None},
    # 页面外观（两个体系共用）：在网页上切换后会自动写回。
    "appearance": {
        "coord_format": "dms",       # 经纬度输入：dms 度分秒（默认）/ decimal 十进制度
    },
    # 版面：各体系结果区标签页的顺序（拖动标签页后自动写回）。
    "layout": {"tab_order": {}},
}

_TAB_ID_RE = re.compile(r"^[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9_-]{0,40}$")

APPEARANCE_CHOICES = {
    "coord_format": ("dms", "decimal"),
}


def _copy(value):
    return json.loads(json.dumps(value))


def deep_merge(base, override):
    result = _copy(base)
    for key, value in (override or {}).items():
        if isinstance(value, Mapping) and isinstance(result.get(key), dict):
            result[key] = deep_merge(result[key], value)
        else:
            result[key] = value
    return result


class SettingsStore:
    def __init__(self, path):
        self.path = Path(path)
        self.data: dict = {}
        self._systems: dict[str, tuple[dict, Callable | None]] = {}
        self._lock = threading.RLock()

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ 体系登记

    def register_system(self, system_id, defaults, normalize=None):
        """normalize(merged_section) -> 整理好的 section；读文件时调用，不合法的内容在这里纠正。"""
        self._systems[system_id] = (_copy(defaults), normalize)

    def system_ids(self):
        return list(self._systems)

    def system(self, system_id) -> dict:
        return self.data["systems"][system_id]

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ 读

    def load(self):
        with self._lock:
            raw = {}
            if self.path.exists():
                raw = json.loads(self.path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
            data = deep_merge(SHARED_DEFAULTS, {k: v for k, v in raw.items() if k != "systems"})
            appearance = data["appearance"]
            # 升级：旧版本的文件里还有 theme（日间 / 夜间）。第一次升级时把经纬度输入改成新的默认值“度分秒”，
            # 并把清理后的外观设置写回文件；之后在网页上的选择照常记住。
            raw_appearance = raw.get("appearance") if isinstance(raw.get("appearance"), dict) else {}
            upgrade_appearance = "theme" in raw_appearance
            if upgrade_appearance:
                appearance["coord_format"] = "dms"
            for key, choices in APPEARANCE_CHOICES.items():
                if appearance.get(key) not in choices:
                    appearance[key] = SHARED_DEFAULTS["appearance"][key]
            for key in list(appearance):
                if key not in APPEARANCE_CHOICES:
                    appearance.pop(key)
            layout = data["layout"] if isinstance(data.get("layout"), dict) else {}
            order = layout.get("tab_order") if isinstance(layout.get("tab_order"), dict) else {}
            data["layout"] = {**layout, "tab_order": {
                str(sid): [str(t) for t in tabs if isinstance(t, str)]
                for sid, tabs in order.items() if isinstance(tabs, list)
            }}
            data["systems"] = {}
            raw_systems = raw.get("systems") if isinstance(raw.get("systems"), dict) else {}
            for sid, (defaults, normalize) in self._systems.items():
                merged = deep_merge(defaults, raw_systems.get(sid) or {})
                data["systems"][sid] = normalize(merged) if normalize else merged
            # 已经不存在的体系，原样保留在文件里（以后装回来还能用），只是不加载。
            self.data = data
            if upgrade_appearance and self.path.exists():
                self.write(("appearance",), dict(appearance), merge=False)
            if not self.path.exists():
                self.path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
                self._dump({**data, "systems": {**raw_systems, **data["systems"]}})
            return data

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ 写

    def _dump(self, payload):
        tmp = self.path.with_suffix(".json.tmp")
        tmp.write_text(json.dumps(payload, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
        tmp.replace(self.path)

    def write(self, keys, values, *, merge=True):
        """把 values 写到 keys 指向的那一节，例如 ("appearance",) 或 ("systems", "western", "points")。

        merge=True 且两边都是 dict 时只更新传来的键；否则整节替换。返回内存里更新后的那一节。
        """
        keys = tuple(keys)
        with self._lock:
            try:
                on_disk = json.loads(self.path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")) if self.path.exists() else {}
            except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
                raise ValueError(f"settings.json 不是合法的 JSON（第 {exc.lineno} 行），设置没有保存。") from exc
            for target in (on_disk, self.data):
                node = target
                for key in keys[:-1]:
                    if not isinstance(node.get(key), dict):
                        node[key] = {}
                    node = node[key]
                last = keys[-1]
                if merge and isinstance(values, dict) and isinstance(node.get(last), dict):
                    node[last].update(_copy(values))
                else:
                    node[last] = _copy(values)
            self._dump(on_disk)
            node = self.data
            for key in keys:
                node = node[key]
            return node

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ 共用的几节

    def save_appearance(self, updates):
        clean = {}
        for key, value in (updates or {}).items():
            if key not in APPEARANCE_CHOICES:
                raise ValueError(f"未知的外观设置：{key}")
            if value not in APPEARANCE_CHOICES[key]:
                raise ValueError(f"外观设置 {key} 只能是 {'、'.join(APPEARANCE_CHOICES[key])}。")
            clean[key] = value
        # 整节写回（merge=False）：顺手清掉旧版本留在文件里、已经不用的外观设置（例如 theme）。
        return dict(self.write(("appearance",), {**self.data.get("appearance", {}), **clean}, merge=False))

    def save_tab_order(self, system_id, order):
        """记住某个体系结果区标签页的顺序（标签页的 id 列表）。"""
        if system_id not in self._systems:
            raise ValueError(f"未知的占星体系：{system_id}")
        if not isinstance(order, list) or len(order) > 60:
            raise ValueError("标签页顺序必须是列表。")
        clean = []
        for item in order:
            if not isinstance(item, str) or not _TAB_ID_RE.match(item):
                raise ValueError(f"标签页的 id 不合法：{item!r}")
            if item not in clean:
                clean.append(item)
        return list(self.write(("layout", "tab_order", system_id), clean, merge=False))

    def save_session(self, updates):
        """updates 可含：library（档案库名）、last_profile（{档案库名: 人物 id 或 null}）、last_system。"""
        session = _copy(self.data.get("session") or {})
        for key, value in (updates or {}).items():
            if key == "library":
                if not isinstance(value, str) or not value.strip():
                    raise ValueError("library 必须是档案库名称。")
                session["library"] = value
            elif key == "last_profile":
                if not isinstance(value, dict):
                    raise ValueError("last_profile 必须是 {档案库: 人物 id}。")
                merged = dict(session.get("last_profile") or {})
                for lib, pid in value.items():
                    if pid is not None and (not isinstance(pid, int) or isinstance(pid, bool) or pid < 1):
                        raise ValueError("人物 id 必须是正整数或 null。")
                    if pid is None:
                        merged.pop(str(lib), None)
                    else:
                        merged[str(lib)] = pid
                session["last_profile"] = merged
            elif key == "last_system":
                if value not in self._systems:
                    raise ValueError(f"未知的占星体系：{value}")
                session["last_system"] = value
            else:
                raise ValueError(f"未知的会话设置：{key}")
        return dict(self.write(("session",), session, merge=False))

    def rename_library_in_session(self, old, new):
        session = _copy(self.data.get("session") or {})
        last = dict(session.get("last_profile") or {})
        if old in last:
            last[new] = last.pop(old)
        session["last_profile"] = last
        if session.get("library") == old:
            session["library"] = new
        self.write(("session",), session, merge=False)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ 导出 / 导入设置
    #
    # 导出的是“计算与显示用的设置”，方便另一个项目组或另一台电脑原样沿用：
    #   共用：default_location、birth_defaults、appearance、layout；
    #   体系：systems.<体系>（计算设置默认值、星体配置、相位、古典设置、星盘样式等）。
    # 不导出 server（各机器的地址端口）和 session（上次用的档案库和人物）。

    EXPORT_FORMAT = "quant-astro-settings"
    EXPORT_VERSION = 1
    EXPORT_SHARED = ("default_location", "birth_defaults", "appearance", "layout")

    def export_bundle(self):
        with self._lock:
            return {
                "format": self.EXPORT_FORMAT,
                "version": self.EXPORT_VERSION,
                "shared": {key: _copy(self.data.get(key)) for key in self.EXPORT_SHARED},
                "systems": {sid: _copy(self.data["systems"][sid]) for sid in self._systems},
            }

    def import_bundle(self, bundle):
        """导入 export_bundle() 导出的内容：只替换文件里出现的那几节，其它设置不动。
        写盘后重新 load()，各节照常经过默认值合并和各体系的整理函数，不合法的内容在那里纠正。
        返回 {"shared": [导入的共用节], "systems": [导入的体系]}。"""
        if not isinstance(bundle, dict) or bundle.get("format") != self.EXPORT_FORMAT:
            raise ValueError("这不是本程序导出的设置文件。")
        if int(bundle.get("version") or 0) > self.EXPORT_VERSION:
            raise ValueError("这个设置文件来自更新版本的程序，请先升级程序再导入。")
        shared = bundle.get("shared") if isinstance(bundle.get("shared"), dict) else {}
        systems = bundle.get("systems") if isinstance(bundle.get("systems"), dict) else {}
        done = {"shared": [], "systems": []}
        with self._lock:
            for key in self.EXPORT_SHARED:
                if isinstance(shared.get(key), dict):
                    self.write((key,), shared[key], merge=False)
                    done["shared"].append(key)
            for sid in self._systems:
                if isinstance(systems.get(sid), dict):
                    self.write(("systems", sid), systems[sid], merge=False)
                    done["systems"].append(sid)
            if not done["shared"] and not done["systems"]:
                raise ValueError("设置文件里没有可导入的内容。")
            self.load()
        return done



########## FILE: common\shell.py ##########
"""页面外壳：首页跳转、两个体系共用的 bootstrap / 外观接口，以及体系页面的渲染。"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from datetime import datetime

from flask import Blueprint, Response, current_app, redirect, render_template, request

from .profiles.driver import LockedError
from .profiles.routes import library_payload, locked_response
from .web import fail, ok

bp = Blueprint("shell", __name__)

_store = None
_libraries = None
_systems: dict = {}


def init_shell(store, libraries, systems):
    global _store, _libraries
    _store = store
    _libraries = libraries
    _systems.clear()
    _systems.update({s.id: s for s in systems})
    return bp


def system_links():
    return [{"id": s.id, "title": s.title, "url": s.url} for s in _systems.values()]


def render_system_page(system_id, template, **context):
    """各体系的页面都用这个函数渲染：自动带上体系切换按钮，并记住“上次用的体系”。"""
    try:
        _store.save_session({"last_system": system_id})
    except (ValueError, OSError):
        pass
    system = _systems[system_id]
    return render_template(
        template,
        systems=system_links(),
        current_system=system_id,
        system_title=system.title,
        api_base=system.api,
        **context,
    )


@bp.get("/")
def home():
    if not _systems:
        return "systems/ 文件夹里没有任何占星体系。", 500
    last = (_store.data.get("session") or {}).get("last_system")
    target = _systems.get(last) or next(iter(_systems.values()))
    return redirect(target.url)


@bp.post("/api/appearance")
def update_appearance():
    try:
        payload = request.get_json(force=True) or {}
        return ok(_store.save_appearance(payload))
    except ValueError as exc:
        return fail(exc, 400)
    except OSError as exc:
        current_app.logger.exception("Saving appearance failed")
        return fail(f"外观设置写入 settings.json 失败：{exc}", 500)


@bp.get("/api/settings/export")
def export_settings():
    """把两个体系的全部计算与显示设置导出成一个 JSON 文件（下载）。"""
    stamp = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d-%H%M")
    body = json.dumps({**_store.export_bundle(), "exported_at": datetime.now().isoformat(timespec="seconds")},
                      ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + "\n"
    return Response(body, mimetype="application/json; charset=utf-8",
                    headers={"Content-Disposition": f"attachment; filename=quant-astro-settings-{stamp}.json"})


@bp.post("/api/settings/import")
def import_settings():
    """上传 export_settings 导出的 JSON，替换其中包含的各节设置。"""
    upload = request.files.get("file")
    if upload is None:
        return fail("没有收到设置文件。", 400)
    try:
        bundle = json.loads(upload.read().decode("utf-8-sig"))
    except (UnicodeDecodeError, json.JSONDecodeError):
        return fail("设置文件不是合法的 JSON。", 400)
    try:
        return ok(_store.import_bundle(bundle))
    except ValueError as exc:
        return fail(exc, 400)
    except OSError as exc:
        current_app.logger.exception("Importing settings failed")
        return fail(f"设置写入 settings.json 失败：{exc}", 500)


@bp.post("/api/layout/tab-order")
def update_tab_order():
    """结果区标签页拖动排序后写回：{"system": 体系 id, "order": [标签页 id, ...]}。"""
    try:
        payload = request.get_json(force=True) or {}
        return ok(_store.save_tab_order(str(payload.get("system") or ""), payload.get("order")))
    except ValueError as exc:
        return fail(exc, 400)
    except OSError as exc:
        current_app.logger.exception("Saving tab order failed")
        return fail(f"标签页顺序写入 settings.json 失败：{exc}", 500)


def shared_settings():
    data = _store.data
    return {key: data[key] for key in ("default_location", "birth_defaults", "session", "appearance", "layout")}


@bp.get("/api/bootstrap")
def bootstrap():
    """打开页面时一次取齐：共用设置 + 某个体系的设置和选项 + 当前档案库第一页。

    ?system=<id> 指定体系。返回的 settings = 共用设置合并体系设置（体系设置里的键优先）。
    """
    system = _systems.get(request.args.get("system", ""))
    if system is None:
        return fail("未知的占星体系。", 400)
    sys_part = system.bootstrap(_store)
    data = {
        "settings": {**shared_settings(), **sys_part["settings"]},
        "options": sys_part["options"],
        "system": {"id": system.id, "title": system.title, "api": system.api},
        "security": _libraries.security_status(),
    }
    try:
        data.update(library_payload())
    except LockedError as exc:
        return locked_response(exc)
    return ok(data, **data)


########## FILE: common\system.py ##########
"""占星体系的挂接接口。

每个体系是 systems/ 下的一个文件夹（Python 包），在 __init__.py 里提供一个 SYSTEM 对象。
程序启动时自动扫描 systems/ 下的所有文件夹并挂上去，所以：
  - 加一个体系 = 放进一个新文件夹；
  - 去掉一个体系 = 删掉它的文件夹（其它体系和共用部分不受影响）；
  - 只把某一个体系交给别的组：给他们 common/、app.py 和 systems/ 下那一个文件夹即可。

一个体系的所有东西（计算、设置、接口、页面、JS、CSS、手册）都在它自己的文件夹里，
只依赖 common/，体系之间互不 import。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import importlib
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Callable

from flask import Blueprint

from .paths import SYSTEMS_DIR


@dataclass
class AstroSystem:
    id: str                                  # 网址和设置里用的名字，如 "vedic"
    title: str                               # 页面上显示的名字，如 "印度占星"
    blueprint: Blueprint                     # 这个体系的页面和接口，挂在 /<id>/ 下
    settings_defaults: dict                  # settings.json → systems.<id> 的默认值
    normalize_settings: Callable | None      # 读设置时的整理函数
    bootstrap: Callable                      # bootstrap(store) -> {"settings": {...}, "options": {...}}
    attach: Callable                         # attach(store, libraries)：启动时把共用对象交给体系
    order: int = 100                         # 顶部切换按钮的顺序
    extra: dict = field(default_factory=dict)

    @property
    def url(self):
        return f"/{self.id}/"

    @property
    def api(self):
        return f"/{self.id}/api"


def discover_systems():
    found = []
    if not SYSTEMS_DIR.is_dir():
        return found
    for folder in sorted(SYSTEMS_DIR.iterdir()):
        if not folder.is_dir() or folder.name.startswith(("_", ".")) or not (folder / "__init__.py").exists():
            continue
        module = importlib.import_module(f"systems.{folder.name}")
        system = getattr(module, "SYSTEM", None)
        if not isinstance(system, AstroSystem):
            raise RuntimeError(f"systems/{folder.name}/__init__.py 里没有 SYSTEM（AstroSystem）。")
        found.append(system)
    found.sort(key=lambda s: (s.order, s.id))
    return found


########## FILE: common\web.py ##########
"""接口返回格式与 JSON 转换（各体系的路由也用这里的 ok / fail）。"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import asdict, is_dataclass
from datetime import date, datetime
from decimal import Decimal
from typing import Mapping

from flask import jsonify


def ok(data=None, **extra):
    payload = {"ok": True}
    if data is not None:
        payload["data"] = safe_json(data)
    payload.update(safe_json(extra))
    return jsonify(payload)


def fail(message, status=400, **extra):
    return jsonify({"ok": False, "error": str(message), **extra}), status


def safe_json(value):
    if value is None or isinstance(value, (str, int, float, bool)):
        return value
    if isinstance(value, Decimal):
        return str(value)
    if isinstance(value, (datetime, date)):
        return value.isoformat()
    if is_dataclass(value):
        return safe_json(asdict(value))
    if isinstance(value, Mapping):
        return {str(k): safe_json(v) for k, v in value.items()}
    if isinstance(value, (list, tuple, set)):
        return [safe_json(v) for v in value]
    if hasattr(value, "to_dict"):
        try:
            return safe_json(value.to_dict())
        except TypeError:
            return safe_json(value.to_dict(json_safe=True))
    return str(value)


########## FILE: common\__init__.py ##########
"""共用区：档案系统（前后端）、天文基础、设置存取、页面外壳。

规则：common 里的任何代码都不 import systems/ 下的任何体系；
各体系只通过 common.system.AstroSystem 这一个接口挂进来。
"""


########## FILE: common\astro\core.py ##########
"""共用的天文基础设施（印度占星、西方占星都用这一份）。

负责：输入解析（时间、时区、经纬度、历法）、Swiss Ephemeris 的星历路径与线程安全、
行星 / 交点位置、宫位与四轴、日出日落、值日星、行星时。

黄道体系只在这里起作用：calculate_planets / calculate_houses 必须显式传入
ecliptic_mode（"tropical" 或 "sidereal"）。输出只是一组黄经数据，各占星体系自己决定怎么用。

依赖方向是单向的：systems/ 下的各占星体系依赖本模块，本模块绝不 import 任何体系的代码。
各体系需要用到的内部帮助函数（_SWISS_LOCK、_calc_body、_position_flags 等）也从这里引用，
改动这些函数时请照顾到所有体系。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import os
import re
import threading
from dataclasses import asdict, dataclass
from datetime import date, datetime, time, timedelta, timezone
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Iterable, Mapping, Sequence

import swisseph as swe


_SWISS_LOCK = threading.RLock()
_ACTIVE_EPHE_PATH: str | None = None

MAIN_PLANETS: dict[str, int] = {
    "Su": swe.SUN,
    "Mo": swe.MOON,
    "Me": swe.MERCURY,
    "Ve": swe.VENUS,
    "Ma": swe.MARS,
    "Ju": swe.JUPITER,
    "Sa": swe.SATURN,
    "Ur": swe.URANUS,
    "Ne": swe.NEPTUNE,
    "Pl": swe.PLUTO,
}

HOUSE_SYSTEMS: dict[str, bytes] = {
    "Placidus": b"P",
    "Koch": b"K",
    "Regiomontanus": b"R",
    "Whole Sign": b"W",
    "Equal": b"E",
    "Campanus": b"C",
    "Alcabitius": b"B",
    "Porphyry": b"O",
    "Morinus": b"M",
    "Topocentric": b"T",
    "Vehlow": b"V",
    "Meridian": b"X",
    "Horizon": b"H",
    "Krusinski": b"U",
    "Carter": b"F",
    "Equal/MC": b"D",
    "Equal/Zodiac": b"N",

# datetime.weekday(): Monday == 0
WEEKDAY_LORDS: dict[int, str] = {
    0: "Mo",
    1: "Ma",
    2: "Me",
    3: "Ju",
    4: "Ve",
    5: "Sa",
    6: "Su",
}
CHALDEAN_ORDER: tuple[str, ...] = ("Sa", "Ju", "Ma", "Su", "Ve", "Me", "Mo")

# 日出日落计算用的大气折射参数。界面上已去掉海拔/气压/气温三个输入项，
# 这里固定使用原来页面的默认值，保证去掉选项前后的日出日落结果一致。
SUNRISE_ELEVATION_M: float = 0.0
SUNRISE_ATPRESS_HPA: float = 1013.25
SUNRISE_ATTEMP_C: float = 20.0


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class AstroContext:
    """一次计算共享的时间与地理底座。

    ``local_dt`` 与 ``utc_dt`` 都是带时区的 datetime；``tz_offset`` 为小时数。
    本地 Web 层需要 JSON 时可使用 :meth:`to_dict`。
    """

    jd_utc: float
    lat: float
    lon: float
    eps: float
    local_dt: datetime
    utc_dt: datetime
    tz_offset: float
    calendar: str = "g"
    ephe_path: str | None = None

    def to_dict(self, *, json_safe: bool = True) -> dict[str, Any]:
        result = asdict(self)
        if json_safe:
            result["local_dt"] = self.local_dt.isoformat()
            result["utc_dt"] = self.utc_dt.isoformat()
        return result


# Swiss Ephemeris 的内部状态（星历路径、岁差模式、打开的文件）在 pysweph 里是“每个线程一份”：
# 在一个线程里 set_ephe_path，另一个线程完全看不到，会退回默认路径
# （小行星、恒星就找不到文件；行星则可能悄悄改用 Moshier）。Flask 是多线程的，
# 每个请求都可能跑在新线程里，所以这里记住“进程级的目标路径”，
# 再在每个线程第一次用到时（或路径变了时）把它真正设置进 Swiss Ephemeris。
_THREAD_STATE = threading.local()
_DEFAULT_EPHE_PATH: str | None = None


# 星历目录：默认是本文件旁边的 ephe/（sefstars.txt、sepl_*.se1、semo_*.se1、seas_*.se1，
# 以及 ast0/、ast1/… 子目录里的单独小行星文件）。也可以用环境变量 ASTRO_EPHE_PATH 指到别处。
EPHE_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent / "ephe"


def _bundled_ephe_path() -> str:
    override = os.environ.get("ASTRO_EPHE_PATH")
    if override and Path(override).is_dir():
        return str(Path(override).expanduser().resolve())
    # 显式记录“无数据目录”状态，防止旧上下文意外沿用后来设置的路径。
    return str(EPHE_DIR) if EPHE_DIR.is_dir() else ""


def set_ephemeris_path(ephe_path: str | Path | None = None) -> str | None:
    """设置星历目录并在当前线程生效；返回实际使用的目录。

    传入路径：之后整个进程默认都用这个目录；不传：用之前设置过的目录，
    从没设置过就用 common/astro/ephe（或环境变量 ASTRO_EPHE_PATH）。
    """
    global _ACTIVE_EPHE_PATH, _DEFAULT_EPHE_PATH
    with _SWISS_LOCK:
        if ephe_path is not None:
            target = "" if str(ephe_path) == "" else str(Path(ephe_path).expanduser().resolve())
            _DEFAULT_EPHE_PATH = target
        else:
            if _DEFAULT_EPHE_PATH is None:
                _DEFAULT_EPHE_PATH = _bundled_ephe_path()
            target = _DEFAULT_EPHE_PATH
        if getattr(_THREAD_STATE, "ephe_path", None) != target:
            swe.set_ephe_path(target)
            _THREAD_STATE.ephe_path = target
        _ACTIVE_EPHE_PATH = target
    return target


def decimal_to_dms(value: float) -> dict[str, float | int | str]:
    """将十进制度数转为结构化 DMS；负号保留在度数与字符串中。"""
    sign = -1 if value < 0 else 1
    absolute = abs(float(value))
    total_centiseconds = round(absolute * 3600.0 * 100.0)
    degrees, remainder = divmod(total_centiseconds, 3600 * 100)
    minutes, centiseconds = divmod(remainder, 60 * 100)
    seconds = centiseconds / 100.0
    signed_degrees = degrees * sign
    prefix = "-" if sign < 0 else ""
    return {
        "d": signed_degrees,
        "m": minutes,
        "s": seconds,
        "str": f'{prefix}{degrees}°{minutes:02d}\'{seconds:05.2f}"',
    }


def _parse_timezone(value: str | float | int) -> float:
    if isinstance(value, (int, float)):
        offset = float(value)
    else:
        text = str(value).strip()
        match = re.fullmatch(r"([+-]?)(\d{1,2})(?::(\d{1,2})|\.(\d+))?", text)
        if not match:
            raise ValueError(f"无法解析时区 {value!r}；支持 +8、-5:30、+5.5。")
        sign = -1.0 if match.group(1) == "-" else 1.0
        hours = float(match.group(2))
        if match.group(3) is not None:
            minutes = int(match.group(3))
            if minutes >= 60:
                raise ValueError("时区分钟必须小于 60。")
            hours += minutes / 60.0
        elif match.group(4) is not None:
            hours = float(f"{match.group(2)}.{match.group(4)}")
        offset = sign * hours
    if not -14.0 <= offset <= 14.0:
        raise ValueError("时区偏移必须在 -14 到 +14 小时之间。")
    return offset


def _parse_coordinate(value: str | float | int, *, latitude: bool) -> float:
    if isinstance(value, (int, float)):
        result = float(value)
    else:
        text = str(value).strip().upper()
        parts = re.findall(r"\d+(?:\.\d+)?", text)
        if not parts:
            raise ValueError(f"无法解析坐标 {value!r}。")
        degrees = float(parts[0])
        minutes = float(parts[1]) if len(parts) > 1 else 0.0
        seconds = float(parts[2]) if len(parts) > 2 else 0.0
        if minutes >= 60 or seconds >= 60:
            raise ValueError(f"坐标分、秒必须小于 60：{value!r}")
        result = degrees + minutes / 60.0 + seconds / 3600.0
        if "-" in text or any(mark in text for mark in ("S", "W", "南", "西")):
            result = -result
    limit = 90.0 if latitude else 180.0
    if not -limit <= result <= limit:
        kind = "纬度" if latitude else "经度"
        raise ValueError(f"{kind}超出有效范围：{result}")
    return result


_CIVIL_RE = re.compile(
    r"^\s*(\d{1,4})-(\d{1,2})-(\d{1,2})[ T](\d{1,2}):(\d{1,2})(?::(\d{1,2})(?:\.(\d{1,6}))?)?\s*$"
)


def _days_in_month(year: int, month: int, calendar: str) -> int:
    if month == 2:
        leap = year % 4 == 0 if calendar == "j" else (year % 4 == 0 and (year % 100 != 0 or year % 400 == 0))
        return 29 if leap else 28
    return 30 if month in (4, 6, 9, 11) else 31


def _parse_civil_datetime(value: str | datetime, calendar: str) -> datetime:
    """把当地钟表时间解析成（格里历的）datetime。

    儒略历的日期按儒略历自己的规则校验（例如 1500-02-29 在儒略历里存在、在格里历里不存在），
    然后换算成格里历；不能先交给 datetime 解析，否则这类日期会被误判为非法。
    """
    cal = calendar.lower()
    if cal not in {"g", "j"}:
        raise ValueError("calendar 只能是 'g'（格里历）或 'j'（儒略历）。")

    if isinstance(value, datetime):
        if value.tzinfo is not None:
            raise ValueError("local_time_str 应是不带时区的当地钟表时间；时区请由 timezone_str 单独传入。")
        if cal == "g":
            return value
        parts = (value.year, value.month, value.day, value.hour, value.minute, value.second, value.microsecond)
    else:
        match = _CIVIL_RE.match(str(value))
        if not match:
            raise ValueError(f"无法解析本地时间 {value!r}，请使用 YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM:SS。")
        y, mo, d, h, mi, sec, frac = match.groups()
        parts = (
            int(y), int(mo), int(d), int(h), int(mi), int(sec or 0),
            int((frac or "0").ljust(6, "0")),
        )

    year, month, day_, hour, minute, second, micro = parts
    if not 1 <= year <= 9999:
        raise ValueError(f"年份 {year} 超出范围（1–9999）。")
    if not 1 <= month <= 12:
        raise ValueError(f"月份 {month} 不合法（1–12）。")
    if not 1 <= day_ <= _days_in_month(year, month, cal):
        name = "儒略历" if cal == "j" else "格里历"
        raise ValueError(f"{name} {year} 年 {month} 月没有 {day_} 日。")
    if not (0 <= hour <= 23 and 0 <= minute <= 59 and 0 <= second <= 59):
        raise ValueError(f"时间 {hour:02d}:{minute:02d}:{second:02d} 不合法。")

    if cal == "g":
        return datetime(year, month, day_, hour, minute, second, micro)

    hour_decimal = hour + minute / 60.0 + second / 3600.0 + micro / 3_600_000_000.0
    jd = swe.julday(year, month, day_, hour_decimal, swe.JUL_CAL)
    g_year, g_month, g_day, g_hour = swe.revjul(jd, swe.GREG_CAL)
    midnight = datetime(int(g_year), int(g_month), int(g_day))
    return midnight + timedelta(hours=g_hour)


def _datetime_to_jd(dt_utc: datetime) -> float:
    """UTC 钟表时间 → 儒略日，直接把 UTC 当作 UT（UT1）交给 swe.julday。

    这与 swetest 的 -ut 参数、以及主流占星软件的做法一致。按定义 |UT1 − UTC| < 0.9 秒。
    不用 swe.utc_to_jd：它是用 Swiss Ephemeris 内置的 ΔT 模型去“推算”UT1，
    对没有实测 ΔT 的近年和未来日期，推算值会偏离真实 UT1（2026 年约偏 0.35 秒，
    越往未来偏得越多），反而让结果和 swetest 对不上。
    """
    dt = dt_utc.astimezone(timezone.utc)
    hour = dt.hour + dt.minute / 60.0 + dt.second / 3600.0 + dt.microsecond / 3_600_000_000.0
    return swe.julday(dt.year, dt.month, dt.day, hour, swe.GREG_CAL)


def _jd_to_local(jd_utc: float, tzinfo: timezone) -> datetime:
    """儒略日 → 当地钟表时间；与 _datetime_to_jd 互为逆运算。"""
    year, month, day_, hour_decimal = swe.revjul(jd_utc, swe.GREG_CAL)
    dt_utc = datetime(int(year), int(month), int(day_), tzinfo=timezone.utc) + timedelta(hours=hour_decimal)
    return dt_utc.astimezone(tzinfo)


def parse_time_geo(
    local_time_str: str | datetime,
    timezone_str: str | float,
    latitude_str: str | float,
    longitude_str: str | float,
    *,
    calendar: str = "g",
    ephe_path: str | Path | None = None,
) -> AstroContext:
    """解析时间与地理输入，返回所有后续函数共享的不可变上下文。"""
    tz_offset = _parse_timezone(timezone_str)
    tzinfo = timezone(timedelta(hours=tz_offset))
    civil_dt = _parse_civil_datetime(local_time_str, calendar)
    local_dt = civil_dt.replace(tzinfo=tzinfo)
    utc_dt = local_dt.astimezone(timezone.utc)
    jd_utc = _datetime_to_jd(utc_dt)
    lat = _parse_coordinate(latitude_str, latitude=True)
    lon = _parse_coordinate(longitude_str, latitude=False)
    with _SWISS_LOCK:
        active_path = set_ephemeris_path(ephe_path)
        eps = float(swe.calc_ut(jd_utc, swe.ECL_NUT, 0)[0][0])
    return AstroContext(
        jd_utc=jd_utc,
        lat=lat,
        lon=lon,
        eps=eps,
        local_dt=local_dt,
        utc_dt=utc_dt,
        tz_offset=tz_offset,
        calendar=calendar.lower(),
        ephe_path=active_path,
    )


def _resolve_ayanamsha(value: str | int) -> int:
    if isinstance(value, int):
        return value
    clean = str(value).replace("swe.", "").strip().upper()
    candidates = (clean, f"SE_{clean}", clean.replace("SIDM_", "SE_SIDM_"))
    for name in candidates:
        if hasattr(swe, name):
            return int(getattr(swe, name))
    raise ValueError(f"找不到岁差模式：{value!r}")


def _position_flags(ecliptic_mode: str, ayanamsha_mode: str | int) -> int:
    mode = ecliptic_mode.lower()
    if mode not in {"tropical", "sidereal"}:
        raise ValueError("ecliptic_mode 只能是 'tropical' 或 'sidereal'。")
    flags = swe.FLG_SWIEPH | swe.FLG_SPEED
    if mode == "sidereal":
        swe.set_sid_mode(_resolve_ayanamsha(ayanamsha_mode))
        flags |= swe.FLG_SIDEREAL
    return flags


def _check_ephemeris_result(
    ret_flag: int | None,
    requested_flags: int,
    label: str,
    serr: str = "",
) -> None:
    """Swiss Ephemeris 星历文件加载不到时直接报错，绝不悄悄回退到 Moshier。"""
    if ret_flag is None or not (requested_flags & swe.FLG_SWIEPH):
        return
    # FLG_MOSEPH 是 pysweph/pyswisseph 的标准常量，不用 getattr(..., 0) 兜底——
    # 那样万一它哪天真的取不到，这个安全检查会悄悄失效而不是报错提醒我们。
    used_moshier = bool(swe.FLG_MOSEPH & ret_flag)
    missing_swiss = not bool(ret_flag & swe.FLG_SWIEPH)
    if not (used_moshier or missing_swiss):
        return
    # pysweph >= 2.10.3.3 起，calc()/calc_ut() 等函数会在返回值里多带一条
    # serr 字符串，通常直接写明具体缺了哪个星历文件，比我们自己拼的提示更准确。
    detail = f"；Swiss Ephemeris 提示：{serr}" if serr else ""
    raise FileNotFoundError(
        f"无法加载 Swiss Ephemeris 星历文件（计算 {label} 时）。"
        f"请确认星历目录里有覆盖该日期的 .se1 文件。当前星历目录：{_ACTIVE_EPHE_PATH!r}{detail}"
    )


def _calc_body(jd_utc: float, body_id: int, flags: int, label: str) -> dict[str, float]:
    ecliptic_result = swe.calc_ut(jd_utc, body_id, flags)
    # 赤经赤纬是天球赤道坐标，与黄道体系无关：必须去掉 FLG_SIDEREAL 再算。
    # 带着恒星黄道标志算出的赤经赤纬会偏离真实值约 15″（不含章动）。
    equatorial_result = swe.calc_ut(jd_utc, body_id, (flags & ~swe.FLG_SIDEREAL) | swe.FLG_EQUATORIAL)
    xx = ecliptic_result[0]
    eq = equatorial_result[0]
    ret_flag = ecliptic_result[1] if len(ecliptic_result) > 1 and isinstance(ecliptic_result[1], int) else None
    # pysweph >= 2.10.3.3 的 calc_ut() 返回 (xx, retflags, serr) 三元组；
    # 旧版 pyswisseph 只有二元组，len(...) > 2 的判断让两种版本都能正常工作。
    serr = ecliptic_result[2] if len(ecliptic_result) > 2 and isinstance(ecliptic_result[2], str) else ""
    _check_ephemeris_result(ret_flag, flags, label, serr=serr)
    return {
        "lon": float(xx[0] % 360.0),
        "lat": float(xx[1]),
        "speed": float(xx[3]),
        "ra": float(eq[0] % 360.0),
        "dec": float(eq[1]),
    }


def calculate_planets(
    context: AstroContext,
    selected_planets: Sequence[str] | None = None,
    *,
    ecliptic_mode: str,
    ayanamsha_mode: str | int = "SIDM_KRISHNAMURTI",
    node_mode: str = "mean",
    ephe_path: str | Path | None = None,
) -> dict[str, dict[str, float]]:
    """仅计算主行星及交点的黄经、纬度、速度、赤经和赤纬。"""
    effective_ephe_path = ephe_path if ephe_path is not None else context.ephe_path
    requested = list(MAIN_PLANETS) + ["Ra", "Ke"] if selected_planets is None or "All" in selected_planets else list(selected_planets)
    unknown = set(requested) - set(MAIN_PLANETS) - {"Ra", "Ke"}
    if unknown:
        raise ValueError(f"未知主行星代码：{sorted(unknown)}")
    node_mode = node_mode.lower()
    if node_mode not in {"mean", "true"}:
        raise ValueError("node_mode 只能是 'mean' 或 'true'。")

    result: dict[str, dict[str, float]] = {}
    with _SWISS_LOCK:
        set_ephemeris_path(effective_ephe_path)
        base_flags = _position_flags(ecliptic_mode, ayanamsha_mode)
        for name in requested:
            if name in {"Ra", "Ke"} or name in result:
                continue
            result[name] = _calc_body(context.jd_utc, MAIN_PLANETS[name], base_flags, name)

        if "Ra" in requested or "Ke" in requested:
            node_id = swe.TRUE_NODE if node_mode == "true" else swe.MEAN_NODE
            north = _calc_body(context.jd_utc, node_id, base_flags, "Ra/Ke")
            if "Ra" in requested:
                result["Ra"] = north
            if "Ke" in requested:
                # 南交点是北交点在天球上的严格反点；直接反转赤道坐标，
                # 避免把恒星黄经误当作热带黄经交给 cotrans。
                result["Ke"] = {
                    "lon": (north["lon"] + 180.0) % 360.0,
                    "lat": -north["lat"],
                    "speed": north["speed"],
                    "ra": (north["ra"] + 180.0) % 360.0,
                    "dec": -north["dec"],
                }
    return {name: result[name] for name in requested if name in result}


def _extract_12(values: Sequence[float]) -> list[float]:
    if len(values) >= 13:
        return [float(value) for value in values[1:13]]
    if len(values) == 12:
        return [float(value) for value in values]
    raise RuntimeError(f"宫位函数返回了非预期长度：{len(values)}")


def _house_flags(ecliptic_mode: str, ayanamsha_mode: str | int) -> int:
    mode = ecliptic_mode.lower()
    if mode == "sidereal":
        swe.set_sid_mode(_resolve_ayanamsha(ayanamsha_mode))
        return swe.FLG_SIDEREAL
    if mode == "tropical":
        return 0
    raise ValueError("ecliptic_mode 只能是 'tropical' 或 'sidereal'。")


def _ecliptic_point(
    longitude: float,
    speed: float,
    eps: float,
    ayanamsha: float,
) -> dict[str, float]:
    """把宫头/轴点统一转换为与行星相同的坐标结构。"""
    longitude %= 360.0
    # houses_ex2 的恒星黄经先加回同一时刻、同一 flags 的岁差值，
    # 再转换为物理上对应点的赤经赤纬。
    tropical_longitude = (longitude + ayanamsha) % 360.0
    equatorial = swe.cotrans((tropical_longitude, 0.0, 1.0), -eps)
    return {
        "lon": longitude,
        "lat": 0.0,
        "speed": float(speed),
        "ra": float(equatorial[0] % 360.0),
        "dec": float(equatorial[1]),
    }


def calculate_houses(
    context: AstroContext,
    house_system: str = "Placidus",
    *,
    ecliptic_mode: str,
    ayanamsha_mode: str | int = "SIDM_KRISHNAMURTI",
    jd_utc: float | None = None,
    ephe_path: str | Path | None = None,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """独立计算 12 宫头与四轴。

    返回结构：
        ``houses``: ``house 1`` 至 ``house 12``；
        ``axes``: Asc / Desc / MC / IC 四轴（任何宫位制下都直接取自 ascmc 计算，
        页面暂不单独展示，但星盘的上升点要用到 Asc）；
        ``extra``: Vertex / AntiVertex / EastPoint（同样取自 ascmc，供特殊点使用）。

    Whole Sign、Equal 等宫位制下，MC 不一定等于第 10 宫宫头，因此四轴
    必须直接取 ``ascmc``，不能从宫头推断。
    """
    if house_system == "Gauquelin":
        raise ValueError("Gauquelin 返回 36 个 sector，不属于 12 宫接口。")
    if house_system not in HOUSE_SYSTEMS:
        raise ValueError(f"未知宫位制 {house_system!r}；可用值：{sorted(HOUSE_SYSTEMS)}")

    target_jd = context.jd_utc if jd_utc is None else float(jd_utc)
    effective_ephe_path = ephe_path if ephe_path is not None else context.ephe_path
    with _SWISS_LOCK:
        # 宫位是可被单独局部刷新的公开入口，因此必须自行恢复星历路径和岁差状态。
        set_ephemeris_path(effective_ephe_path)
        flags = _house_flags(ecliptic_mode, ayanamsha_mode)
        raw = swe.houses_ex2(
            target_jd,
            context.lat,
            context.lon,
            HOUSE_SYSTEMS[house_system],
            flags=flags,
        )
        cusps = _extract_12(raw[0])
        ascmc = [float(value) for value in raw[1]]
        cusp_speeds = _extract_12(raw[2])
        ascmc_speeds = [float(value) for value in raw[3]]
        target_eps = float(swe.calc_ut(target_jd, swe.ECL_NUT, 0)[0][0])
        ayanamsha = (
            float(swe.get_ayanamsa_ex_ut(target_jd, flags)[1])
            if ecliptic_mode.lower() == "sidereal"
            else 0.0
        )

        houses = {
            f"house {index}": _ecliptic_point(longitude, speed, target_eps, ayanamsha)
            for index, (longitude, speed) in enumerate(zip(cusps, cusp_speeds), start=1)
        }

        asc = _ecliptic_point(ascmc[0], ascmc_speeds[0], target_eps, ayanamsha)
        mc = _ecliptic_point(ascmc[1], ascmc_speeds[1], target_eps, ayanamsha)
        desc = _ecliptic_point(
            (ascmc[0] + 180.0) % 360.0,
            ascmc_speeds[0],
            target_eps,
            ayanamsha,
        )
        ic = _ecliptic_point(
            (ascmc[1] + 180.0) % 360.0,
            ascmc_speeds[1],
            target_eps,
            ayanamsha,
        )
        axes = {"Asc": asc, "Desc": desc, "MC": mc, "IC": ic}

        # ascmc[3] = Vertex，ascmc[4] = 赤道上升点（East Point）。与四轴一样，
        # 任何宫位制下 houses_ex2 都会给出，与所选宫位制无关。
        vertex = _ecliptic_point(ascmc[3], ascmc_speeds[3], target_eps, ayanamsha)
        extra = {
            "Vertex": vertex,
            "AntiVertex": _ecliptic_point(
                (ascmc[3] + 180.0) % 360.0, ascmc_speeds[3], target_eps, ayanamsha
            ),
            "EastPoint": _ecliptic_point(ascmc[4], ascmc_speeds[4], target_eps, ayanamsha),
        }


    return {
        "house_system": house_system,
        "ecliptic_mode": ecliptic_mode.lower(),
        "jd_utc": target_jd,
        "houses": houses,
        "axes": axes,
        "extra": extra,
    }


def _coerce_date(value: date | datetime | str | None, default: date) -> date:
    if value is None:
        return default
    if isinstance(value, datetime):
        return value.date()
    if isinstance(value, date):
        return value
    return date.fromisoformat(str(value))


def _sun_event(
    context: AstroContext,
    target_date: date,
    event_flag: int,
    style_flags: int,
) -> tuple[float, datetime]:
    local_midnight = datetime.combine(target_date, time.min, tzinfo=context.local_dt.tzinfo)
    jd_start = _datetime_to_jd(local_midnight.astimezone(timezone.utc))
    result_flag, event_times = swe.rise_trans(
        jd_start,
        swe.SUN,
        event_flag | style_flags,
        (context.lon, context.lat, SUNRISE_ELEVATION_M),
        SUNRISE_ATPRESS_HPA,
        SUNRISE_ATTEMP_C,
        swe.FLG_SWIEPH,
    )
    event_jd = float(event_times[0])
    if result_flag < 0 or event_jd <= 1.0:
        event_name = "日出" if event_flag == swe.CALC_RISE else "日落"
        raise ValueError(f"{target_date} 无法计算{event_name}（可能处于极昼/极夜区域）。")
    return event_jd, _jd_to_local(event_jd, context.local_dt.tzinfo)


def calculate_sunrise_sunset(
    context: AstroContext,
    target_date: date | datetime | str | None = None,
    *,
    rsmi: int | None = None,
    ephe_path: str | Path | None = None,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """统一、精确计算指定当地日期的日出和日落。"""
    effective_ephe_path = ephe_path if ephe_path is not None else context.ephe_path
    local_date = _coerce_date(target_date, context.local_dt.date())
    user_flags = swe.BIT_DISC_CENTER if rsmi is None else int(rsmi)
    style_flags = user_flags & ~(swe.CALC_RISE | swe.CALC_SET)
    with _SWISS_LOCK:
        set_ephemeris_path(effective_ephe_path)
        # rise_trans 找不到星历文件时会悄悄改用 Moshier，所以先用太阳试算一次，缺文件就直接报错。
        probe = swe.calc_ut(context.jd_utc, swe.SUN, swe.FLG_SWIEPH)
        _check_ephemeris_result(
            probe[1] if len(probe) > 1 and isinstance(probe[1], int) else None,
            swe.FLG_SWIEPH, "日出日落",
            serr=probe[2] if len(probe) > 2 and isinstance(probe[2], str) else "",
        )
        rise_jd, sunrise = _sun_event(context, local_date, swe.CALC_RISE, style_flags)
        set_jd, sunset = _sun_event(context, local_date, swe.CALC_SET, style_flags)
    if sunrise.date() != local_date or sunset.date() != local_date:
        raise ValueError(
            f"{local_date} 当地并非同时存在日出和日落（可能处于极昼/极夜过渡期）；"
            "不会用相邻日期事件冒充当天事件。"
        )
    return {
        "date": local_date.isoformat(),
        "sunrise_local": sunrise,
        "sunset_local": sunset,
        "sunrise_jd_utc": rise_jd,
        "sunset_jd_utc": set_jd,
    }


def calculate_day_lord(
    context: AstroContext,
    sun_events: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
    *,
    rsmi: int | None = None,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """按“日出换日”规则计算迦勒底值日星。"""
    events = dict(sun_events) if sun_events is not None else calculate_sunrise_sunset(context, rsmi=rsmi)
    sunrise = events["sunrise_local"]
    if isinstance(sunrise, str):
        sunrise = datetime.fromisoformat(sunrise)
    before_sunrise = context.local_dt < sunrise
    astrological_date = context.local_dt.date() - timedelta(days=1 if before_sunrise else 0)
    return {
        "day_lord": WEEKDAY_LORDS[astrological_date.weekday()],
        "astrological_date": astrological_date.isoformat(),
        "is_before_sunrise": before_sunrise,
        "sunrise_local": sunrise,
    }


def calculate_planetary_hour(
    context: AstroContext,
    *,
    rsmi: int | None = None,
    ephe_path: str | Path | None = None,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """按昼、夜各十二等分计算当前行星时。"""
    effective_ephe_path = ephe_path if ephe_path is not None else context.ephe_path
    today = calculate_sunrise_sunset(context, rsmi=rsmi, ephe_path=effective_ephe_path)
    sunrise_today = today["sunrise_local"]
    sunset_today = today["sunset_local"]

    if context.local_dt < sunrise_today:
        previous = calculate_sunrise_sunset(
            context, context.local_dt.date() - timedelta(days=1), rsmi=rsmi, ephe_path=effective_ephe_path
        )
        period_start, period_end = previous["sunset_local"], sunrise_today
        astrological_date = context.local_dt.date() - timedelta(days=1)
        daytime = False
        base_offset = 12
    elif context.local_dt >= sunset_today:
        following = calculate_sunrise_sunset(
            context, context.local_dt.date() + timedelta(days=1), rsmi=rsmi, ephe_path=effective_ephe_path
        )
        period_start, period_end = sunset_today, following["sunrise_local"]
        astrological_date = context.local_dt.date()
        daytime = False
        base_offset = 12
    else:
        period_start, period_end = sunrise_today, sunset_today
        astrological_date = context.local_dt.date()
        daytime = True
        base_offset = 0

    hour_seconds = (period_end - period_start).total_seconds() / 12.0
    elapsed = max(0.0, (context.local_dt - period_start).total_seconds())
    period_index = min(11, int(elapsed / hour_seconds))
    hour_number = base_offset + period_index + 1
    day_lord = WEEKDAY_LORDS[astrological_date.weekday()]
    lord_index = CHALDEAN_ORDER.index(day_lord)
    planetary_lord = CHALDEAN_ORDER[(lord_index + hour_number - 1) % 7]
    hour_start = period_start + timedelta(seconds=period_index * hour_seconds)
    hour_end = period_start + timedelta(seconds=(period_index + 1) * hour_seconds)

    return {
        "planetary_hour_lord": planetary_lord,
        "day_lord": day_lord,
        "astrological_date": astrological_date.isoformat(),
        "is_day_time": daytime,
        "hour_number": hour_number,
        "period_hour": period_index + 1,
        "hour_start": hour_start,
        "hour_end": hour_end,
        "hour_length_seconds": hour_seconds,
    }


########## FILE: common\astro\options.py ##########
"""两个体系共用的下拉选项：岁差、宫位制。"""

from __future__ import annotations

import swisseph as swe

from .core import HOUSE_SYSTEMS


def ayanamsha_options():
    """直接从当前安装的 pysweph 里枚举全部预定义岁差（SIDM_*），不手写清单。

    这样 pysweph 将来新增岁差时，下拉菜单会自动跟着多出来，不会遗漏。
    - SIDM_USER 需要额外提供 t0 / ayan_t0 两个参数，不是一个现成的“预设”，所以不放进下拉菜单。
    - NSIDM_PREDEF 只是“预定义岁差总数”的计数值，不是岁差，同样不列入。
    """
    found = []
    for name in dir(swe):
        if not name.startswith("SIDM_") or name == "SIDM_USER":
            continue
        value = getattr(swe, name)
        if not isinstance(value, int):
            continue
        try:
            title = str(swe.get_ayanamsa_name(value) or "").strip()
        except Exception:
            title = ""
        found.append((value, name, title))
    found.sort()
    return [{"value": name, "label": title or name} for _, name, title in found]


def house_system_options():
    return list(HOUSE_SYSTEMS.keys())


BASIC_CALC_KEYS = ("ecliptic_mode", "ayanamsha_mode", "house_system", "node_mode")


def clean_basic_calc(updates):
    """校验计算设置里两个体系共有的四项（黄道、岁差、宫位制、月交点）。"""
    choices = {
        "ecliptic_mode": ("tropical", "sidereal"),
        "ayanamsha_mode": tuple(o["value"] for o in ayanamsha_options()),
        "house_system": tuple(HOUSE_SYSTEMS),
        "node_mode": ("mean", "true"),
    }
    clean = {}
    for key, value in (updates or {}).items():
        if key not in choices:
            raise ValueError(f"未知的计算设置：{key}")
        if value not in choices[key]:
            raise ValueError(f"计算设置 {key} 的值不合法：{value}")
        clean[key] = value
    return clean

########## FILE: common\astro\ephe\sefstars.txt ##########
# sefstars.txt: Swiss Ephemeris fixed stars data file, 
# Fixed stars brighter than Magnitude 5 
# This file is based on data provided by S. Moshier.
# It was later increased and improved by Valentin Abramov and Dieter Koch
# (see history further below).
#
# The data in a star record are:
# traditional name (no leading blanks in field)
# nomenclature name (no leading blanks in field)
# equinox (must be either '1950' or '2000' or 'ICRS')
# right ascension hours
#               minutes
#               seconds
# declination   degrees
#               minutes
#               seconds
# proper motion in right asc., 0.001"/year * cos(decl0)
#               in decl., 0.001"/year
# radial velocity in km/s
# annual parallax in 0.001"
# magnitude V
# DM zone (Durchmusterung zone; declination 1900)
# DM number 
# The last two parameters are not supported and are not required for 
# the file to be valid (since SE 2.07).
#
# To achieve higher speed for swe_fixstar(), you can copy the 
# stars you prefer to the top of the list
#
# File editing History:
# ---------------------
# 7 May 1999:
# File was improved by Valentin Abramov, Tartu, Estonia in April 1999.
# - The stars are ordered by constellation.
# - Most names come from Arabic and have several transliterations, 
#   e.g. Algieba and Al Jabhah. In such cases, the record has been 
#   duplicated and both spellings have been given.
# 
# 11 Jan 2006, Dieter Koch:
# File updated with data from the SIMBAD Astronomical Database.
# http://simbad.u-strasbg.fr/simbad/sim-fid
# 
# The Galactic Center was taken from 
# The Astrophysical Journal, Volume 518, Issue 1, pp. L33-L35.
#
# 22 May 2016, DK:
# File updated with data from the SIMBAD Astronomical Database.
# http://simbad.u-strasbg.fr/simbad/sim-fid
#
# 1 Nov 2016, DK:
# 110 stars added
#
# 4 Jan 2018, DK:
# Some wrong traditional names corrected.
# Names added from https://www.iau.org/public/themes/naming_stars/
# "List of IAU-approved Star Names as of 19 November 2017"
# File updated with data from the SIMBAD Astronomical Database.
#
# 2-mar-24 AT
# added Gal.Equ which is only precise in year 2000, 
# see https://groups.io/g/swisseph/message/12192
# ---------------------------------------------
#
Aldebaran  ,alTau,ICRS,04,35,55.23907,+16,30,33.4885,63.45,-188.94,54.26,48.94,0.86, 16,  629
Rohini  ,alTau,ICRS,04,35,55.23907,+16,30,33.4885,63.45,-188.94,54.26,48.94,0.86, 16,  629

########## FILE: common\profiles\crypto.py ##########
"""档案库加密（SQLCipher）。

每个档案库可以单独加密，各有各的密码。加密后的库仍是 user_data/libraries/<名字>.sqlite3，
只是整个文件按页用 AES-256 加密（SQLCipher 4 默认参数：每页 4096 字节、HMAC-SHA512 校验、
PBKDF2-HMAC-SHA512 256000 次迭代推导密钥）。读写都直接在磁盘上按页进行，不需要把整库载入内存，
几十万份档案和未加密时一样流畅。WAL 日志文件同样是密文。

- 程序只在内存里记住已解锁库的密码，关掉程序即全部锁上。密码不写进任何文件。
  **忘记密码，这个档案库无法找回**。
- 用 DB Browser for SQLite（选 SQLCipher 4 默认设置）输入同一个密码也能直接打开加密库。
- 需要 sqlcipher3（pip install sqlcipher3-wheels）。没装时加密功能不可用，其它功能照常。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import os
from pathlib import Path

from .driver import HAS_SQLCIPHER, PLAIN_HEADER, WrongPassword, apply_key, sqlite

MIN_PASSWORD = 6


def crypto_available():
    return HAS_SQLCIPHER


def is_encrypted(path):
    """文件头不是 "SQLite format 3" 的非空文件视为 SQLCipher 加密库。"""
    path = Path(path)
    try:
        if path.stat().st_size == 0:
            return False
        with path.open("rb") as fh:
            return fh.read(16) != PLAIN_HEADER
    except OSError:
        return False


def check_new_password(password):
    if not isinstance(password, str) or len(password) < MIN_PASSWORD:
        raise ValueError(f"密码至少 {MIN_PASSWORD} 个字符。")
    if not HAS_SQLCIPHER:
        raise ValueError("加密功能需要 sqlcipher3 库：请先运行 pip install sqlcipher3-wheels，然后重新启动程序。")


def verify(path, key):
    """核对密码；不对时抛 WrongPassword。"""
    conn = sqlite.connect(str(path))
    try:
        apply_key(conn, key)
    finally:
        conn.close()


def remove_sqlite_files(path):
    for suffix in ("", "-wal", "-shm", "-journal"):
        p = Path(str(path) + suffix)
        if p.exists():
            p.unlink()


def _export(path, old_key, new_key):
    """把 path 整库导出到同目录的临时文件（old_key → new_key；None 表示不加密），再替换原文件。

    先把原库切回普通日志模式（合并并删掉 -wal），确保替换后不会有旧的 WAL 文件残留在新库旁边。
    导出完成之前原文件不动，中途出错时原库完好。
    """
    if not HAS_SQLCIPHER:
        raise ValueError("加密功能需要 sqlcipher3 库：请先运行 pip install sqlcipher3-wheels。")
    path = Path(path)
    tmp = path.with_name(path.name + ".tmp")
    remove_sqlite_files(tmp)
    conn = sqlite.connect(str(path), isolation_level=None)
    try:
        if old_key is not None:
            apply_key(conn, old_key)
        conn.execute("PRAGMA journal_mode = DELETE")
        version = int(conn.execute("PRAGMA user_version").fetchone()[0])
        conn.execute("ATTACH DATABASE ? AS target KEY ?", (str(tmp), new_key or ""))
        conn.execute("SELECT sqlcipher_export('target')")
        conn.execute(f"PRAGMA target.user_version = {version}")
        conn.execute("DETACH DATABASE target")
    except BaseException:
        conn.close()
        remove_sqlite_files(tmp)
        raise
    conn.close()
    for suffix in ("-wal", "-shm", "-journal"):
        leftover = Path(str(path) + suffix)
        if leftover.exists():
            leftover.unlink()
    os.replace(tmp, path)


def encrypt_file(path, password):
    check_new_password(password)
    _export(path, None, password)


def decrypt_file(path, password):
    verify(path, password)
    _export(path, password, None)


def rekey_file(path, old, new):
    check_new_password(new)
    verify(path, old)
    _export(path, old, new)


__all__ = ["WrongPassword", "check_new_password", "crypto_available", "decrypt_file", "encrypt_file",
           "is_encrypted", "rekey_file", "remove_sqlite_files", "verify"]


########## FILE: common\profiles\db.py ##########
from __future__ import annotations

from contextlib import contextmanager
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path
import csv
import io
import json
import math
import re
import threading
import unicodedata

from .driver import apply_key, sqlite as sqlite3

# 必备字段（人名、时间、历法、地点）直接是 people 表的列，不属于“自定义字段”，
# 因此既不能被删除，也不允许用同名再建一个自定义字段。
# CSV 的固定列名也在这里，避免自定义字段与它们重名、导入时分不清。
CORE_FIELD_LABELS = {
    "id", "人物名称", "人物姓名", "姓名", "名称",
    "出生时间", "时间", "本地时间", "历法", "时区", "UTC 时区",
    "纬度", "经度", "地点", "出生地点",
    "标签", "分类标签", "创建时间", "修改时间",
}

MAX_LABEL_LEN = 40
MAX_VALUE_LEN = 20000
MAX_TAG_LEN = 30
MAX_TAGS_PER_PROFILE = 20
MAX_OPTION_LEN = 30
MAX_OPTIONS = 200
MAX_FIELD_FILTERS = 10

SCHEMA_VERSION = 6
MAX_FIELD_DEPTH = 5          # 字段最多嵌套 5 层
PATH_SEP = "/"               # 字段路径的分隔符（CSV 表头写成 婚姻/是否二婚），字段名里不能出现
POOL_SIZE = 4                # 每个档案库保留的空闲连接数

DEFAULT_PAGE_SIZE = 50
MAX_PAGE_SIZE = 200

# 历法：起盘的必备要素之一。
CALENDARS = {"g": "格里历", "j": "儒略历"}

# 自定义字段的类型：
#   text    文本（可多行，如长篇分析）。原文参与全文检索。
#   number  数字。另存解析出的数值 num_value，并建 (字段, 数值) 索引：可排序、可按范围筛选。
#   date    日期。可写 2020-05-12、2020-05 或 2020；num_value 存 YYYYMMDD 整数
#           （缺月/日记 00），同样可排序、可按范围筛选。
#   choice  单选。选项事先定义好，只能选一个；选中的选项另存进 profile_choices 并建索引。
#   multi   多选。同上，可以选多个。
#   group   分组。自己不存值，只用来收纳下级字段（例如“婚姻”下面放“是否二婚”“结婚次数”）。
# 任何字段都可以有下级字段（parent_id 指向上级），最多 MAX_FIELD_DEPTH 层。
# 数据库里只放“人物相关的数据”和“排盘必要的要素”，不记录星座、度数之类的星盘结果。
FIELD_KINDS = {
    "text": "文本",
    "number": "数字",
    "date": "日期",
    "choice": "单选",
    "multi": "多选",
    "group": "分组",
}
NUMERIC_KINDS = frozenset({"number", "date"})     # 有 num_value，可排序、可范围筛选
CHOICE_KINDS = frozenset({"choice", "multi"})      # 有 profile_choices，可按选项筛选

# 多选值、标签在文本里的分隔符（显示时用“、”连接）。
_LIST_SPLIT_RE = re.compile(r"[,，、;；\n]+")
_OPTION_FORBIDDEN = set(",，、;；\n")


# ====================================================================== 解析工具
# 这些都是不依赖数据库的纯函数。

def _norm(text):
    """全角→半角、统一负号，并去掉首尾空白。"""
    s = unicodedata.normalize("NFKC", str(text))
    return s.replace("−", "-").replace("–", "-").strip()


_THOUSANDS_RE = re.compile(r"[+-]?\d{1,3}(?:,\d{3})+(?:\.\d+)?")
_NUM_RE = re.compile(r"[+-]?(?:\d+(?:\.\d*)?|\.\d+)(?:[eE][+-]?\d+)?")


def parse_number(text):
    """"12" "-3.5" "1,024" "１２" → 数字；其他写法（含 nan/inf）一律视为无法识别。"""
    s = _norm(text)
    if not s:
        return None
    if _THOUSANDS_RE.fullmatch(s):
        s = s.replace(",", "")
    if not _NUM_RE.fullmatch(s):
        return None
    value = float(s)
    return value if math.isfinite(value) else None


def is_leap_year(year, calendar):
    if calendar == "j":
        return year % 4 == 0
    return year % 4 == 0 and (year % 100 != 0 or year % 400 == 0)


def days_in_month(year, month, calendar):
    if month == 2:
        return 29 if is_leap_year(year, calendar) else 28
    return 30 if month in (4, 6, 9, 11) else 31


def _jdn(year, month, day, calendar):
    a = (14 - month) // 12
    y = year + 4800 - a
    m = month + 12 * a - 3
    base = day + (153 * m + 2) // 5 + 365 * y + y // 4
    return base - 32083 if calendar == "j" else base - y // 100 + y // 400 - 32045


def _gregorian_from_jdn(jdn):
    a = jdn + 32044
    b = (4 * a + 3) // 146097
    c = a - 146097 * b // 4
    d = (4 * c + 3) // 1461
    e = c - 1461 * d // 4
    m = (5 * e + 2) // 153
    day = e - (153 * m + 2) // 5 + 1
    month = m + 3 - 12 * (m // 10)
    year = 100 * b + d - 4800 + m // 10
    return year, month, day


# 出生时间：接受 1990-05-12 08:30:00、1990/5/12 8:30、1990-05-12T08:30 等写法
# （Excel 另存 CSV 时常把日期改成斜杠、去掉前导零或秒）。
_BIRTH_RE = re.compile(
    r"^\s*(\d{1,4})[-/.年](\d{1,2})[-/.月](\d{1,2})日?(?:[ T]+(\d{1,2})[:：时](\d{1,2})(?:[:：分](\d{1,2})(?:\.\d+)?秒?)?)?\s*$"
)


def parse_birth_time(text, calendar="g"):
    """出生时间文本 → (规范文本, YYYYMMDD, 当天第几秒)；不合法返回 None。

    规范文本按原历法写成 “YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM:SS”。YYYYMMDD 是换算到格里历后的日期，
    这样格里历、儒略历的档案混在一起时，按出生先后排序、按年份筛选仍然正确。
    """
    m = _BIRTH_RE.match(_norm(text))
    if not m:
        return None
    y, mo, d = int(m.group(1)), int(m.group(2)), int(m.group(3))
    h, mi, s = int(m.group(4) or 0), int(m.group(5) or 0), int(m.group(6) or 0)
    if not (1 <= y <= 9999 and 1 <= mo <= 12 and 1 <= d <= days_in_month(y, mo, calendar)):
        return None
    if not (0 <= h <= 23 and 0 <= mi <= 59 and 0 <= s <= 59):
        return None
    gy, gm, gd = (y, mo, d) if calendar == "g" else _gregorian_from_jdn(_jdn(y, mo, d, "j"))
    canonical = f"{y:04d}-{mo:02d}-{d:02d} {h:02d}:{mi:02d}:{s:02d}"
    return canonical, gy * 10000 + gm * 100 + gd, h * 3600 + mi * 60 + s


def clean_calendar(value):
    text = _norm(value or "g").lower()
    aliases = {"g": "g", "greg": "g", "gregorian": "g", "格里历": "g", "公历": "g", "阳历": "g",
               "j": "j", "jul": "j", "julian": "j", "儒略历": "j"}
    if text not in aliases:
        raise ValueError(f"历法只能是 g（格里历）或 j（儒略历），收到：{value!r}")
    return aliases[text]


_TZ_RE = re.compile(r"^(?:UTC|GMT)?\s*([+-])?(\d{1,2})(?::(\d{2})|\.(\d+))?$", re.IGNORECASE)


def clean_timezone(value):
    """时区：+08:00 / -5:30 / +5.5 / UTC+8 → 规范成 ±HH:MM；超出 ±14 小时报错。"""
    text = _norm(value).replace(" ", "")
    m = _TZ_RE.match(text)
    if not m:
        raise ValueError(f"时区写法不对：{value!r}，请写成 +08:00、-05:30 这样的形式。")
    sign = -1 if m.group(1) == "-" else 1
    hours = int(m.group(2))
    if m.group(3) is not None:
        minutes = int(m.group(3))
    elif m.group(4) is not None:
        minutes = round(float("0." + m.group(4)) * 60)
    else:
        minutes = 0
    if minutes >= 60 or hours * 60 + minutes > 14 * 60:
        raise ValueError(f"时区超出范围：{value!r}")
    return f"{'+' if sign > 0 else '-'}{hours:02d}:{minutes:02d}"


_DATE_RE = re.compile(r"^\s*(\d{1,4})(?:\s*[-/.年]\s*(\d{1,2}))?(?:\s*[-/.月]\s*(\d{1,2}))?\s*日?\s*$")


def parse_date_value(text):
    """日期字段：2020-05-12 / 2020-05 / 2020 / 2020/5/12 / 2020年5月 → (规范文本, YYYYMMDD 数值)。"""
    m = _DATE_RE.match(_norm(text))
    if not m:
        return None
    y = int(m.group(1))
    mo = int(m.group(2)) if m.group(2) else None
    d = int(m.group(3)) if m.group(3) else None
    if not 1 <= y <= 9999:
        return None
    if mo is not None and not 1 <= mo <= 12:
        return None
    if d is not None and not 1 <= d <= days_in_month(y, mo, "g"):
        return None
    canonical = f"{y:04d}" + (f"-{mo:02d}" if mo else "") + (f"-{d:02d}" if d else "")
    return canonical, float(y * 10000 + (mo or 0) * 100 + (d or 0))


def derive_num(kind, text):
    """按字段类型把原文解析成数值；不能解析时返回 None。"""
    if kind == "number":
        return parse_number(text)
    if kind == "date":
        parsed = parse_date_value(text)
        return parsed[1] if parsed else None
    return None


_PERIOD_RE = re.compile(r"^\s*(\d{1,4})(?:\s*[-/.]\s*(\d{1,2}))?(?:\s*[-/.]\s*(\d{1,2}))?\s*$")


def period_bound(text, *, end):
    """日期范围筛选的边界：'1980' / '1980-05' / '1980-05-12' → YYYYMMDD 整数。

    作为起点取这段时间的最前面（缺月 / 日记 00，这样只写了年份的日期字段也会被包含），
    作为终点取最后一天，所以 1980～1989 就是“1980 年初到 1989 年底”。
    """
    m = _PERIOD_RE.match(_norm(text))
    if not m:
        raise ValueError(f"日期筛选格式不对：{text!r}，请用 1980、1980-05 或 1980-05-12。")
    year = int(m.group(1))
    month = int(m.group(2)) if m.group(2) else None
    day = int(m.group(3)) if m.group(3) else None
    if month is not None and not 1 <= month <= 12:
        raise ValueError(f"日期筛选里的月份不对：{text!r}")
    if day is not None and not 1 <= day <= 31:
        raise ValueError(f"日期筛选里的日不对：{text!r}")
    if end:
        return year * 10000 + (month or 12) * 100 + (day or 31)
    return year * 10000 + (month or 0) * 100 + (day or 0)


def split_list(text):
    """“A、B; C” → ['A', 'B', 'C']（去空、去重，不分大小写）。"""
    items = [text] if isinstance(text, str) else list(text or [])
    result, seen = [], set()
    for item in items:
        for piece in _LIST_SPLIT_RE.split(str(item)):
            name = " ".join(piece.split())
            if name and name.casefold() not in seen:
                seen.add(name.casefold())
                result.append(name)
    return result


_CJK_RUN_RE = re.compile(r"[\u3400-\u4dbf\u4e00-\u9fff\uf900-\ufaff\U00020000-\U0002fa1f]+")


def cjk_bigrams(text):
    """给“两字词索引”用：把连续的汉字拆成相邻两字一组（“迁居海外” → “迁居 居海 海外”），
    单个汉字原样保留，其它文字不变。FTS5 的 unicode61 分词器再按空格和标点切开。"""
    if not text:
        return ""

    def split(match):
        run = match.group(0)
        if len(run) == 1:
            return f" {run} "
        return " " + " ".join(run[i:i + 2] for i in range(len(run) - 1)) + " "

    return _CJK_RUN_RE.sub(split, str(text))


def _is_cjk_pair(term):
    return len(term) == 2 and bool(_CJK_RUN_RE.fullmatch(term))


def _search_terms(q):
    """全文检索的关键词：空格分开的每个词都必须出现（AND）。"""
    return [t for t in _norm(q).split() if t][:8]


def _excerpt(text, term, width=36):
    """在 text 里找到 term，截取它前后一段作为摘要。"""
    pos = text.casefold().find(term.casefold())
    if pos < 0:
        return None
    start = max(0, pos - width // 2)
    end = min(len(text), pos + len(term) + width)
    snippet = " ".join(text[start:end].split())
    return ("…" if start > 0 else "") + snippet + ("…" if end < len(text) else "")


# ======================================================================= 数据库

class ProfileDB:
    """人物档案库（一个 SQLite 文件；加密时是 SQLCipher 文件，接口完全相同）。

    表结构（schema v6）：
      people           必备字段：名称、出生时间、历法、时区、纬度、经度、创建/更新时间；
                       birth_ymd / birth_tod 是由出生时间派生出的整数（换算到格里历），
                       用来做按日期范围筛选和按出生先后排序。
      profile_fields   自定义字段的定义：字段名、类型 kind、单选/多选的选项 options（JSON 数组）、
                       上级字段 parent_id（顶层为 NULL）、同级之间的顺序 position。
                       字段树只是几十到几百行的元数据，嵌套多少层都不影响档案数据的读写速度。
      profile_values   每个人在每个字段上的取值（不管字段在第几层，都是一行）：
                         value      原文（单选 / 多选是用“、”连接的选项文本）
                         num_value  数字 / 日期解析出的数值，其它类型为 NULL
      profile_choices  单选 / 多选字段选中的选项（一人一字段可以多行），按 (字段, 选项) 建索引
      tags             分类标签（名称唯一，不分大小写）
      people_tags      人物 ↔ 标签（多对多）
      profile_fts      全文检索索引（SQLite FTS5，trigram 分词，支持中文任意 3 字以上的片段）：
                       每人一行，内容 = 名字 + 标签 + 全部字段原文。
                       SQLite 不支持 trigram 时自动退回逐条模糊匹配，功能不变、只是慢一些。

    索引原则：
      建索引：人名、出生日期、修改时间、纬度/经度、(字段, 数值)、(字段, 选项)、(标签, 人物)、全文索引。
      profile_values.value 本身不建普通索引，长文只进全文索引。

    连接：每个库保留一个小连接池（最多 POOL_SIZE 个空闲连接，多线程共用）。
    加密库每打开一个新连接都要做一次密钥推导（约 0.1–0.2 秒），连接池让这笔开销只发生几次；
    未加密库也因此能复用各连接的页缓存。close() 关闭全部连接（改名、锁定、加解密之前调用）。
    """

    def __init__(self, path, key=None):
        self.path = Path(path)
        self.path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        self.key = key
        self.fts = False
        self._pool = []
        self._pool_lock = threading.Lock()
        self._closed = False
        self._init_db()

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ 连接

    def _connect(self):
        conn = sqlite3.connect(
            str(self.path),
            timeout=10.0,
            isolation_level=None,      # 自动提交模式；需要事务时手动 BEGIN/COMMIT
            check_same_thread=False,   # 连接池里的连接会被不同的请求线程轮流使用（同一时刻只有一个线程在用）
        )
        try:
            if self.key is not None:
                apply_key(conn, self.key)
            conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
            conn.create_function("qa_bigrams", 1, cjk_bigrams, deterministic=True)
            conn.execute("PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON")
            # WAL：读不阻塞写、写不阻塞读（Flask 是多线程的），提交时磁盘同步次数也更少。
            # 运行期间旁边会多出 -wal / -shm 两个文件，属正常现象，程序关闭时自动合并回主文件。
            conn.execute("PRAGMA journal_mode = WAL")
            conn.execute("PRAGMA synchronous = NORMAL")
            conn.execute("PRAGMA temp_store = MEMORY")
            conn.execute("PRAGMA cache_size = -32768")  # 每个连接最多 32 MB 页缓存
        except BaseException:
            conn.close()
            raise
        return conn

    def _acquire(self):
        with self._pool_lock:
            if self._pool:
                return self._pool.pop()
        return self._connect()

    def _release(self, conn):
        try:
            if conn.in_transaction:
                conn.execute("ROLLBACK")
        except sqlite3.Error:
            conn.close()
            return
        with self._pool_lock:
            if not self._closed and len(self._pool) < POOL_SIZE:
                self._pool.append(conn)
                return
        conn.close()

    def close(self):
        """关闭全部空闲连接；之后正在使用的连接用完也会直接关闭。"""
        with self._pool_lock:
            self._closed = True
            pool, self._pool = self._pool, []
        for conn in pool:
            conn.close()

    @contextmanager
    def _read(self):
        conn = self._acquire()
        try:
            yield conn
        finally:
            self._release(conn)

    @contextmanager
    def _tx(self):
        """写事务：成功则提交，任何异常则整体回滚。"""
        conn = self._acquire()
        try:
            conn.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
            try:
                yield conn
            except BaseException:
                conn.execute("ROLLBACK")
                raise
            else:
                conn.execute("COMMIT")
        finally:
            self._release(conn)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------ 建表

    _INDEXES = (
        "CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_people_display_name "
        "ON people(display_name COLLATE NOCASE)",
        "CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_people_birth "
        "ON people(birth_ymd, birth_tod)",
        "CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_people_updated "
        "ON people(updated_at)",
        "CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_people_latitude "
        "ON people(latitude)",
        "CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_people_longitude "
        "ON people(longitude)",
        # 同一个上级下面字段名不能重复（不同上级下可以都有“详情”）。
        "CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_profile_fields_sibling_label "
        "ON profile_fields(COALESCE(parent_id, 0), label COLLATE NOCASE)",
        "CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_profile_fields_parent "
        "ON profile_fields(parent_id)",
        # 同时服务：按字段数值排序/筛选、“未填写”筛选、统计某字段被多少人填过、删除字段时的级联清理。
        # 末尾带上 profile_id，让这些查询只读索引（覆盖索引），不必回表去读整行（行里可能有上万字的长文）：
        # 20 万份档案时，按数值筛选从约 0.3 秒（加密库约 1.2 秒）降到几毫秒。
        "CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_pv_field_num_profile "
        "ON profile_values(field_id, num_value, profile_id)",
        "CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_pc_field_option "
        "ON profile_choices(field_id, option COLLATE NOCASE, profile_id)",
        "CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_tags_name "
        "ON tags(name COLLATE NOCASE)",
        "CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_people_tags_tag "
        "ON people_tags(tag_id, profile_id)",
    )

    _TABLES = (
        """
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS people (
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
            display_name TEXT NOT NULL,
            birth_time TEXT NOT NULL,
            timezone_offset TEXT NOT NULL,
            latitude REAL NOT NULL,
            longitude REAL NOT NULL,
            created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
            updated_at TEXT NOT NULL,
            birth_ymd INTEGER,
            birth_tod INTEGER,
            calendar TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'g'
        )
        """,
        """
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS profile_fields (
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
            label TEXT NOT NULL,
            created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
            kind TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'text',
            options TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]',
            position INTEGER,
            parent_id INTEGER REFERENCES profile_fields(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
        )
        """,
        """
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS profile_values (
            profile_id INTEGER NOT NULL
                REFERENCES people(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
            field_id INTEGER NOT NULL
                REFERENCES profile_fields(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
            value TEXT NOT NULL,
            num_value REAL,
            PRIMARY KEY (profile_id, field_id)
        )
        """,
        """
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS profile_choices (
            profile_id INTEGER NOT NULL
                REFERENCES people(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
            field_id INTEGER NOT NULL
                REFERENCES profile_fields(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
            option TEXT NOT NULL,
            PRIMARY KEY (profile_id, field_id, option)
        ) WITHOUT ROWID
        """,
        """
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS tags (
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
            name TEXT NOT NULL
        )
        """,
        """
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS people_tags (
            profile_id INTEGER NOT NULL
                REFERENCES people(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
            tag_id INTEGER NOT NULL
                REFERENCES tags(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
            PRIMARY KEY (profile_id, tag_id)
        ) WITHOUT ROWID
        """,
    )

    def _init_db(self):
        with self._tx() as conn:
            version = int(conn.execute("PRAGMA user_version").fetchone()[0])
            for ddl in self._TABLES:
                conn.execute(ddl)
            # 开发期的旧库（schema v5）只差字段的上级一列和新的唯一索引；数据不做其它迁移。
            columns = {row["name"] for row in conn.execute("PRAGMA table_info(profile_fields)")}
            if "parent_id" not in columns:
                conn.execute("ALTER TABLE profile_fields ADD COLUMN parent_id INTEGER "
                             "REFERENCES profile_fields(id) ON DELETE CASCADE")
            conn.execute("DROP INDEX IF EXISTS idx_profile_fields_label")
            conn.execute("DROP INDEX IF EXISTS idx_pv_field_num")
            conn.execute("UPDATE profile_fields SET position = id WHERE position IS NULL")
            for ddl in self._INDEXES:
                conn.execute(ddl)

            # 全文索引：需要 SQLite 3.34+ 的 trigram 分词；不支持时退回逐条模糊匹配。
            try:
                conn.execute(
                    "CREATE VIRTUAL TABLE IF NOT EXISTS profile_fts "
                    "USING fts5(content, tokenize = 'trigram')"
                )
                # 两字词索引：trigram 至少要 3 个字才能用上索引，而中文最常见的是两字词（离异、晚婚……）。
                # 这里存的是 cjk_bigrams() 拆好的文本，只用来查两个汉字的词，不存原文（content=''）。
                try:
                    conn.execute(
                        "CREATE VIRTUAL TABLE IF NOT EXISTS profile_bigram "
                        "USING fts5(content, content = '', contentless_delete = 1, tokenize = 'unicode61')"
                    )
                except sqlite3.OperationalError:      # SQLite 3.43 以前不支持 contentless_delete：改存原文
                    conn.execute(
                        "CREATE VIRTUAL TABLE IF NOT EXISTS profile_bigram "
                        "USING fts5(content, tokenize = 'unicode61')"
                    )
                self.fts = True
            except sqlite3.OperationalError:
                self.fts = False
            if self.fts and (version < SCHEMA_VERSION or self._fts_out_of_sync(conn)):
                self._rebuild_fts(conn)

            conn.execute(f"PRAGMA user_version = {SCHEMA_VERSION}")

        if version < SCHEMA_VERSION:
            # 刚建好的库：采集一次统计信息，让查询计划从第一天起就是对的。
            with self._read() as conn:
                conn.execute("ANALYZE")

    def optimize(self):
        """大批量导入之后调用：更新查询统计信息。"""
        with self._read() as conn:
            conn.execute("PRAGMA optimize")

    # ------------------------------------------------------------ 全文索引
    #
    # 一个人的全文内容 = 名字 + 标签 + 全部字段原文（按字段 id 顺序）。
    # 用一条 INSERT … SELECT 成批生成：保存一份档案时只处理这一人，
    # 删字段、改标签影响到几万人时也只是几条集合运算，不会逐人往返查询。

    _FTS_CONTENT_SQL = """
        SELECT p.id,
               p.display_name
               || COALESCE(char(10) || (SELECT group_concat(name, char(10)) FROM (
                      SELECT t.name AS name FROM people_tags pt JOIN tags t ON t.id = pt.tag_id
                      WHERE pt.profile_id = p.id ORDER BY t.name)), '')
               || COALESCE(char(10) || (SELECT group_concat(value, char(10)) FROM (
                      SELECT v.value AS value FROM profile_values v
                      WHERE v.profile_id = p.id ORDER BY v.field_id)), '')
        FROM people AS p
    """

    @staticmethod
    def _fts_out_of_sync(conn):
        """用外部工具直接改过数据库后，全文索引可能和 people 表对不上：人数不同就整体重建。"""
        people = conn.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM people").fetchone()[0]
        indexed = conn.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM profile_fts").fetchone()[0]
        bigram = conn.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM profile_bigram").fetchone()[0]
        return not people == indexed == bigram

    def _refresh_fts(self, conn, profile_ids):
        if not self.fts:
            return
        ids = sorted({int(p) for p in profile_ids})
        for start in range(0, len(ids), 500):
            chunk = ids[start:start + 500]
            marks = ",".join("?" * len(chunk))
            conn.execute(f"DELETE FROM profile_fts WHERE rowid IN ({marks})", chunk)
            conn.execute(f"DELETE FROM profile_bigram WHERE rowid IN ({marks})", chunk)
            conn.execute(
                f"INSERT INTO profile_fts (rowid, content) {self._FTS_CONTENT_SQL} WHERE p.id IN ({marks})", chunk
            )
            conn.execute(
                f"INSERT INTO profile_bigram (rowid, content) "
                f"SELECT rowid, qa_bigrams(content) FROM profile_fts WHERE rowid IN ({marks})", chunk
            )

    def _rebuild_fts(self, conn):
        conn.execute("DELETE FROM profile_fts")
        conn.execute("DELETE FROM profile_bigram")
        conn.execute(f"INSERT INTO profile_fts (rowid, content) {self._FTS_CONTENT_SQL}")
        conn.execute("INSERT INTO profile_bigram (rowid, content) SELECT rowid, qa_bigrams(content) FROM profile_fts")

    # --------------------------------------------------------------- 小工具

    @staticmethod
    def _now():
        return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()

    @staticmethod
    def _int_arg(value, name, default, lo, hi):
        if value is None or value == "":
            return default
        try:
            number = int(value)
        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            raise ValueError(f"{name} 必须是整数。")
        return max(lo, min(hi, number))

    @staticmethod
    def _like(text):
        """把用户输入变成 LIKE 的“包含”模式，并转义 % _ \\ 这三个特殊字符。"""
        escaped = (
            str(text).strip()
            .replace("\\", "\\\\").replace("%", "\\%").replace("_", "\\_")
        )
        return f"%{escaped}%"

    @staticmethod
    def _validate(data):
        display_name = " ".join(str(data.get("display_name", "")).split())
        if not display_name:
            raise ValueError("人物名称不能为空。")

        calendar = clean_calendar(data.get("calendar", "g"))

        birth_text = str(data.get("birth_time", "")).strip()
        if not birth_text:
            raise ValueError("出生时间不能为空。")
        parsed = parse_birth_time(birth_text, calendar)
        if parsed is None:
            raise ValueError(
                f"出生时间「{birth_text}」不合法（{CALENDARS[calendar]}）：请写成 YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM[:SS]，"
                "并确认这一天在该历法里存在。"
            )

        if not str(data.get("timezone_offset", "")).strip():
            raise ValueError("时区不能为空。")
        timezone_offset = clean_timezone(data.get("timezone_offset"))

        try:
            latitude = float(data.get("latitude"))
            longitude = float(data.get("longitude"))
        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            raise ValueError("经纬度必须是数字。")
        if not -90 <= latitude <= 90:
            raise ValueError("纬度必须位于 -90 到 90 之间。")
        if not -180 <= longitude <= 180:
            raise ValueError("经度必须位于 -180 到 180 之间。")

        return {
            "display_name": display_name,
            "birth_time": parsed[0],
            "birth_ymd": parsed[1],
            "birth_tod": parsed[2],
            "calendar": calendar,
            "timezone_offset": timezone_offset,
            "latitude": latitude,
            "longitude": longitude,
        }

    @staticmethod
    def _clean_label(label):
        label = " ".join(str(label or "").split())
        if not label:
            raise ValueError("字段名不能为空。")
        if len(label) > MAX_LABEL_LEN:
            raise ValueError(f"字段名不能超过 {MAX_LABEL_LEN} 个字符。")
        if label in CORE_FIELD_LABELS or label.casefold() == "id":
            raise ValueError(f"「{label}」是必备字段或保留名称，不能当作自定义字段名。")
        if PATH_SEP in label:
            raise ValueError(f"字段名里不能有「{PATH_SEP}」（它用来分隔上下级字段）。")
        return label

    @staticmethod
    def _clean_kind(kind):
        kind = str(kind).strip().lower()
        if kind not in FIELD_KINDS:
            raise ValueError(
                "字段类型只能是：" + "、".join(f"{k}（{v}）" for k, v in FIELD_KINDS.items())
            )
        return kind

    @staticmethod
    def _clean_option(name):
        name = " ".join(_norm(name).split())
        if not name:
            raise ValueError("选项不能为空。")
        if len(name) > MAX_OPTION_LEN:
