########## FILE: systems\western\astro\aspects.py ##########
"""相位计算：在两组点之间（或一组点内部）找出所有落在容许度内的相位。

本模块不调用 Swiss Ephemeris，只用已经算好的黄经和每日速度：
- 角距：两点黄经差折算到 0–180°。
- 偏差：角距与相位角度之差的绝对值，也就是“离精确相位还差几度”。
- 入相 / 出相：按两点的速度看偏差此刻是在变小（入相，A）还是在变大（出相，S）；
  两点相对静止（例如恒星对朔望点）或刚好精确时不标。
- 容许度：每个点有自己的容许度，两个点之间用哪个由合成规则决定（见 combine_orb）。
  标记为 exclusive 的点（例如恒星）不参与合成，始终只用它自己的容许度。
- 同一对点同时落在两种相位的容许范围内时，两种都返回。

调用方负责决定哪些点参与、每个点的容许度，以及要跳过哪些固定的点对。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Callable, Iterable, Sequence

#: 两点之间容许度的合成规则。
ORB_RULES: dict[str, str] = {
    "moiety": "古典半径法：两个点的容许度各取一半再相加",
    "max": "取两者中较大的容许度",
    "min": "取两者中较小的容许度",
}

# 相对速度小于这个值（度/日）视为相对静止，不判断入相 / 出相。
_STATIC_SPEED = 1e-7
# 偏差小于这个值（度，约 0.04 角秒）视为精确。
_EXACT = 1e-5


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class AspectPoint:
    id: str
    lon: float
    speed: float
    orb: float
    exclusive: bool = False


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class AspectType:
    id: str
    name: str
    angle: float
    symbol: str


def combine_orb(a: AspectPoint, b: AspectPoint, rule: str) -> float:
    if a.exclusive and b.exclusive:
        return min(a.orb, b.orb)
    if a.exclusive:
        return a.orb
    if b.exclusive:
        return b.orb
    if rule == "max":
        return max(a.orb, b.orb)
    if rule == "min":
        return min(a.orb, b.orb)
    return (a.orb + b.orb) / 2.0          # moiety


def _separation(lon_a: float, lon_b: float) -> tuple[float, int]:
    """角距（0–180°），以及 b 相对 a 的黄经差增大时角距的变化方向（+1 / -1）。"""
    delta = (lon_b - lon_a) % 360.0
    if delta <= 180.0:
        return delta, 1
    return 360.0 - delta, -1


def _sign_ok(lon_a: float, lon_b: float, angle: float) -> bool:
    """不跨星座：只认星座之间也构成同一相位的情况（只对 30° 整数倍的相位有意义）。"""
    if abs(angle / 30.0 - round(angle / 30.0)) > 1e-9:
        return True
    diff = (int(lon_b // 30) - int(lon_a // 30)) % 12
    diff = min(diff, 12 - diff)
    return abs(diff * 30.0 - angle) < 1e-9


def aspect_between(
    a: AspectPoint,
    b: AspectPoint,
    types: Sequence[AspectType],
    *,
    rule: str,
    cross_sign: bool = True,
) -> list[dict]:
    """两个点之间所有成立的相位（可能不止一个），按偏差从小到大排列。"""
    sep, direction = _separation(a.lon % 360.0, b.lon % 360.0)
    orb = combine_orb(a, b, rule)
    rel_speed = b.speed - a.speed
    found = []
    for t in types:
        dev = sep - t.angle
        if abs(dev) > orb + 1e-12:
            continue
        if not cross_sign and not _sign_ok(a.lon % 360.0, b.lon % 360.0, t.angle):
            continue
        # d(角距)/dt = direction × 相对速度；d|偏差|/dt = sign(偏差) × d(角距)/dt
        if abs(rel_speed) < _STATIC_SPEED or abs(dev) < _EXACT:
            motion = None
        else:
            change = (1.0 if dev > 0 else -1.0) * direction * rel_speed
            motion = "A" if change < 0 else "S"
        found.append({
            "type": t.id,
            "name": t.name,
            "symbol": t.symbol,
            "angle": t.angle,
            "separation": sep,
            "deviation": abs(dev),
            "orb": orb,
            "motion": motion,
        })
    found.sort(key=lambda x: x["deviation"])
    return found


def find_aspects(
    group_a: Sequence[AspectPoint],
    group_b: Sequence[AspectPoint] | None,
    types: Iterable[AspectType],
    *,
    rule: str = "moiety",
    cross_sign: bool = True,
    skip: Callable[[AspectPoint, AspectPoint], bool] | None = None,
) -> list[dict]:
    """两组点之间的相位。group_b 为 None 时计算 group_a 内部两两之间的相位（每对只算一次）。

    返回的每一项：a、b 两个点的 id，以及 aspect_between 给出的相位信息。
    """
    if rule not in ORB_RULES:
        raise ValueError(f"未知的容许度合成规则：{rule}")
    types = list(types)
    if not types:
        return []
    pairs: list[tuple[AspectPoint, AspectPoint]] = []
    if group_b is None:
        for i, b in enumerate(group_a):
            for a in group_a[:i]:
                pairs.append((a, b))
    else:
        pairs = [(a, b) for a in group_a for b in group_b if a.id != b.id]
    result = []
    for a, b in pairs:
        if skip and skip(a, b):
            continue
        for hit in aspect_between(a, b, types, rule=rule, cross_sign=cross_sign):
            result.append({"a": a.id, "b": b.id, **hit})
    return result


########## FILE: systems\western\astro\dignities.py ##########
"""古典占星：先天尊贵、得时、速度、太阳光束。

由一颗行星的黄经、速度，以及整张盘的日夜和太阳位置，算出行星表的各列：
- 三分主星、界主星、面主星
- 先天尊贵记分：入庙、擢升、三分、界、面加分；失势、入弱、外来减分
- 速度与平均速度的比较
- 是否得时、东出 / 西入
- 与太阳的关系：日核、焦伤、光束下

所有可选的规则都由设置给出（settings.json → systems.western.classical，见 bridge.py），
本模块不调用 Swiss Ephemeris，只用已经算好的位置。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import math
from typing import Any, Mapping

CLASSICAL = ("Su", "Mo", "Me", "Ve", "Ma", "Ju", "Sa")
SECT_PLANETS = ("Su", "Mo", "Me", "Ve", "Ma", "Ju", "Sa", "Ur", "Ne", "Pl")

# 入庙（传统七曜）
DOMICILE = ("Ma", "Ve", "Me", "Mo", "Su", "Me", "Ve", "Ma", "Ju", "Sa", "Sa", "Ju")

# 擢升：行星 → (星座, 擢升度数)
EXALTATION = {"Su": (0, 19), "Mo": (1, 3), "Me": (5, 15), "Ve": (11, 27), "Ma": (9, 28), "Ju": (3, 15), "Sa": (6, 21)}

# 三分主星：元素（0 火 1 土 2 风 3 水）→ (日间主星, 夜间主星, 共管)
# 共管可以是一颗星，也可以是 (日间共管, 夜间共管)。
TRIPLICITY = {
    # 多罗修斯（Dorotheus，《占星诗集》）：日、夜、共管三位。
    "dorothean": {0: ("Su", "Ju", "Sa"), 1: ("Ve", "Mo", "Ma"), 2: ("Sa", "Me", "Ju"), 3: ("Ve", "Ma", "Mo")},
    # 托勒密（《四书》I.18）：火、土、风只有日夜两位；水象由火星统辖，金星（日）、月亮（夜）共管。
    "ptolemaic": {0: ("Su", "Ju", None), 1: ("Ve", "Mo", None), 2: ("Sa", "Me", None), 3: ("Ma", "Ma", ("Ve", "Mo"))},
    # Lilly（《基督教占星》的尊贵表）：只有日夜两位，水象日夜都是火星。
    "lilly": {0: ("Su", "Ju", None), 1: ("Ve", "Mo", None), 2: ("Sa", "Me", None), 3: ("Ma", "Ma", None)},
}

# 界：每个星座 5 段，(主星, 这一段结束的度数)
TERMS = {
    "egyptian": (
        (("Ju", 6), ("Ve", 12), ("Me", 20), ("Ma", 25), ("Sa", 30)),
        (("Ve", 8), ("Me", 14), ("Ju", 22), ("Sa", 27), ("Ma", 30)),
        (("Me", 6), ("Ju", 12), ("Ve", 17), ("Ma", 24), ("Sa", 30)),
        (("Ma", 7), ("Ve", 13), ("Me", 19), ("Ju", 26), ("Sa", 30)),
        (("Ju", 6), ("Ve", 11), ("Sa", 18), ("Me", 24), ("Ma", 30)),
        (("Me", 7), ("Ve", 17), ("Ju", 21), ("Ma", 28), ("Sa", 30)),
        (("Sa", 6), ("Me", 14), ("Ju", 21), ("Ve", 28), ("Ma", 30)),
        (("Ma", 7), ("Ve", 11), ("Me", 19), ("Ju", 24), ("Sa", 30)),
        (("Ju", 12), ("Ve", 17), ("Me", 21), ("Sa", 26), ("Ma", 30)),
        (("Me", 7), ("Ju", 14), ("Ve", 22), ("Sa", 26), ("Ma", 30)),
        (("Me", 7), ("Ve", 13), ("Ju", 20), ("Ma", 25), ("Sa", 30)),
        (("Ve", 12), ("Ju", 16), ("Me", 19), ("Ma", 28), ("Sa", 30)),
    ),
    # 托勒密界（Lilly《基督教占星》所载）。
    "ptolemaic": (
        (("Ju", 6), ("Ve", 14), ("Me", 21), ("Ma", 26), ("Sa", 30)),
        (("Ve", 8), ("Me", 15), ("Ju", 22), ("Sa", 26), ("Ma", 30)),
        (("Me", 7), ("Ju", 14), ("Ve", 21), ("Sa", 25), ("Ma", 30)),
        (("Ma", 6), ("Ju", 13), ("Me", 20), ("Ve", 27), ("Sa", 30)),
        (("Sa", 6), ("Me", 13), ("Ve", 19), ("Ju", 25), ("Ma", 30)),
        (("Me", 7), ("Ve", 13), ("Ju", 18), ("Sa", 24), ("Ma", 30)),
        (("Sa", 6), ("Ve", 11), ("Ju", 19), ("Me", 24), ("Ma", 30)),
        (("Ma", 6), ("Ju", 14), ("Ve", 21), ("Me", 27), ("Sa", 30)),
        (("Ju", 8), ("Ve", 14), ("Me", 19), ("Sa", 25), ("Ma", 30)),
        (("Ve", 6), ("Me", 12), ("Ju", 19), ("Ma", 25), ("Sa", 30)),
        (("Sa", 6), ("Me", 12), ("Ve", 20), ("Ju", 25), ("Ma", 30)),
        (("Ve", 8), ("Ju", 14), ("Me", 20), ("Ma", 26), ("Sa", 30)),
    ),
}

# 面（decan）：迦勒底顺序，从白羊第一面的火星开始连续排下去。
FACE_SEQUENCE = ("Ma", "Su", "Ve", "Me", "Mo", "Sa", "Ju")

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- 平均速度（度 / 日）
#
# astronomical：平均黄经运动。外行星、月亮取 J2000 平均根数的变化率（Simon et al. 1994 / Meeus），
#   是相对固定春分点（≈ 恒星背景）的运动；回归黄道再加上岁差 50.29″/年（0.00003824°/日）。
#   水星、金星的地心平均运动等于太阳。冥王星取公转周期 90560 日。
# traditional：Lilly《基督教占星》的平均日行（太阳、水星、金星 59′08″，月亮 13°10′36″，
#   火星 31′27″，木星 4′59″，土星 2′01″）；外行星和交点没有传统数值，用 astronomical。
# empirical：Swiss Ephemeris 1900–2100 年逐日速度绝对值的平均（逆行段按大小计入），
#   数值明显大于平均黄经运动：例如土星 0.0695，约是 0.0335 的两倍。

PRECESSION_PER_DAY = 1.39688783 / 36525.0

MEAN_FIXED = {
    "Su": 0.98560912, "Mo": 13.17635823, "Me": 0.98560912, "Ve": 0.98560912, "Ma": 0.52403284,
    "Ju": 0.08309119, "Sa": 0.03345965, "Ur": 0.01173079, "Ne": 0.00598183, "Pl": 0.00397527,
    "Ra": 0.05299199, "Ke": 0.05299199,
}

TRADITIONAL_MEAN = {
    "Su": 59 / 60 + 8 / 3600, "Mo": 13 + 10 / 60 + 36 / 3600, "Me": 59 / 60 + 8 / 3600, "Ve": 59 / 60 + 8 / 3600,
    "Ma": 31 / 60 + 27 / 3600, "Ju": 4 / 60 + 59 / 3600, "Sa": 2 / 60 + 1 / 3600,
}

EMPIRICAL_MEAN = {
    "Su": 0.985646, "Mo": 13.176443, "Me": 1.217580, "Ve": 1.040696, "Ma": 0.566033,
    "Ju": 0.133057, "Sa": 0.069513, "Ur": 0.033381, "Ne": 0.021237, "Pl": 0.017200,
}
EMPIRICAL_NODE = {"mean": 0.052954, "true": 0.059264}

SPEED_PRESETS = ("astronomical", "traditional", "empirical", "custom")


def astronomical_mean(code: str, *, sidereal: bool) -> float:
    base = MEAN_FIXED[code]
    if code in ("Ra", "Ke"):
        return base if sidereal else base - PRECESSION_PER_DAY     # 交点退行：回归黄道下慢一点
    return base if sidereal else base + PRECESSION_PER_DAY


def mean_speed(code: str, speed_cfg: Mapping[str, Any], *, sidereal: bool, node_mode: str) -> float | None:
    if code not in MEAN_FIXED:
        return None
    preset = speed_cfg.get("preset", "astronomical")
    if preset == "custom":
        value = (speed_cfg.get("custom") or {}).get(code)
        if isinstance(value, (int, float)) and value > 0:
            return float(value)
    elif preset == "traditional" and code in TRADITIONAL_MEAN:
        return TRADITIONAL_MEAN[code]
    elif preset == "empirical":
        if code in ("Ra", "Ke"):
            return EMPIRICAL_NODE["true" if node_mode == "true" else "mean"]
        return EMPIRICAL_MEAN[code]
    return astronomical_mean(code, sidereal=sidereal)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- 基础

_EPS = 1e-9


def norm360(lon: float) -> float:
    return float(lon) % 360.0


def sign_index(lon: float) -> int:
    return int(math.floor(norm360(lon) / 30.0 + _EPS)) % 12


def degree_in_sign(lon: float) -> float:
    x = norm360(lon)
    deg = x - sign_index(x) * 30.0
    return deg if 0.0 <= deg < 30.0 else 0.0


def dms(value: float, *, signed: bool = False) -> str:
    """十进制度 → 度分秒（截断到整秒）。"""
    v = float(value)
    total = int(math.floor(abs(v) * 3600.0 + 1e-6))
    sign = "-" if v < 0 and total else ("+" if signed else "")
    d, rest = divmod(total, 3600)
    m, s = divmod(rest, 60)
    return f"{sign}{d}°{m:02d}′{s:02d}″"


def position(lon: float) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """星座 + 星座内度分秒。"""
    return {"sign_index": sign_index(lon), "dms": dms(degree_in_sign(lon))}


def triplicity_rulers(sign: int, system: str, is_day: bool) -> dict[str, str | None]:
    day, night, part = TRIPLICITY.get(system, TRIPLICITY["dorothean"])[sign % 4]
    if isinstance(part, tuple):
        part = part[0] if is_day else part[1]
    return {"day": day, "night": night, "participating": part}


def term_ruler(lon: float, system: str = "egyptian") -> str:
    table = TERMS.get(system, TERMS["egyptian"])[sign_index(lon)]
    deg = degree_in_sign(lon)
    for ruler, end in table:
        if deg < end:
            return ruler
    return table[-1][0]


def face_ruler(lon: float) -> str:
    decan = min(2, int(degree_in_sign(lon) // 10))
    return FACE_SEQUENCE[(sign_index(lon) * 3 + decan) % 7]


def angular_distance(a: float, b: float) -> float:
    d = abs(norm360(a) - norm360(b)) % 360.0
    return 360.0 - d if d > 180.0 else d


def is_oriental(lon: float, sun_lon: float) -> bool:
    """东出：行星比太阳先升起，即黄经落在太阳之前 0–180°。"""
    diff = (norm360(sun_lon) - norm360(lon)) % 360.0
    return 0.0 < diff < 180.0


def planet_sect(code: str, rule: str | None, *, oriental: bool | None, is_day: bool) -> str | None:
    """行星的昼夜派别：'diurnal' / 'nocturnal' / None（不参与）。

    rule：diurnal 日间派、nocturnal 夜间派、none 不参与；
          oriental 东出为日间派、西入为夜间派；chart 随盘（日生盘算日间派，夜生盘算夜间派）。
    """
    if rule in ("diurnal", "nocturnal"):
        return rule
    if rule == "oriental":
        return None if oriental is None else ("diurnal" if oriental else "nocturnal")
    if rule == "chart":
        return "diurnal" if is_day else "nocturnal"
    return None


def essential_dignity(code: str, lon: float, *, is_day: bool, cfg: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    """传统七曜的先天尊贵记分；外行星、交点返回 None。"""
    if code not in CLASSICAL:
        return None
    scores = cfg.get("scores") or {}
    sign = sign_index(lon)
    items: list[tuple[str, float]] = []
    positive = False

    def add(key: str, positive_item: bool) -> None:
        nonlocal positive
        items.append((key, float(scores.get(key, 0))))
        positive = positive or positive_item

    if DOMICILE[sign] == code:
        add("domicile", True)
    if EXALTATION.get(code, (None,))[0] == sign:
        add("exaltation", True)
    t = triplicity_rulers(sign, cfg.get("triplicity_system", "dorothean"), is_day)
    mode = cfg.get("triplicity_mode", "sect")
    if mode == "all":
        trip = {t["day"], t["night"], t["participating"]}
    elif mode == "both":
        trip = {t["day"], t["night"]}
    else:
        trip = {t["day"] if is_day else t["night"]}
    if code in trip:
        add("triplicity", True)
    if term_ruler(lon, cfg.get("term_system", "egyptian")) == code:
        add("term", True)
    if face_ruler(lon) == code:
        add("face", True)
    if DOMICILE[(sign + 6) % 12] == code:
        add("detriment", False)
    if EXALTATION.get(code, (None,))[0] == (sign + 6) % 12:
        add("fall", False)
    if not positive:
        add("peregrine", False)
    total = sum(v for _, v in items)
    return {"score": int(total) if float(total).is_integer() else round(total, 2), "items": items}


def solar_phase(code: str, lon: float, sun_lon: float | None, cfg: Mapping[str, Any]) -> str | None:
    """与太阳的关系，返回设置里对应那一档的名称（例如“日核”“焦伤”“光束下”）。

    cfg["phases"] 是使用者自定义的若干档 [{"label", "orb"}]，按距离从小到大排好：
    行星与太阳的黄经距离落在哪一档的度数以内，就取最先满足的那一档。
    太阳、交点，以及比最大一档还远的，返回 None。
    """
    if sun_lon is None or code in ("Su", "Ra", "Ke"):
        return None
    d = angular_distance(lon, sun_lon)
    for phase in cfg.get("phases") or []:
        if d <= float(phase["orb"]):
            return str(phase["label"])
    return None


def planet_rows(
    planet_pos: Mapping[str, Mapping[str, Any]],
    *,
    is_day: bool,
    cfg: Mapping[str, Any],
    sidereal: bool,
    node_mode: str,
) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    """页面行星表的每一行。cfg 即设置里的 classical 一节。"""
    sun_lon = planet_pos.get("Su", {}).get("lon")
    sect_rules = (cfg.get("sect") or {}).get("planets") or {}
    speed_cfg = cfg.get("speed") or {}
    rows = []
    for code, p in planet_pos.items():
        lon = norm360(p["lon"])
        speed = float(p.get("speed") or 0.0)
        oriental = None if sun_lon is None or code in ("Su", "Ra", "Ke") else is_oriental(lon, sun_lon)
        sect = planet_sect(code, sect_rules.get(code), oriental=oriental, is_day=is_day)
        mean = mean_speed(code, speed_cfg, sidereal=sidereal, node_mode=node_mode)
        compared = speed if speed_cfg.get("compare") == "signed" else abs(speed)
        rows.append({
            "name": code,
            **position(lon),
            "triplicity": triplicity_rulers(sign_index(lon), cfg.get("triplicity_system", "dorothean"), is_day),
            "term": term_ruler(lon, cfg.get("term_system", "egyptian")),
            "face": face_ruler(lon),
            "dignity": essential_dignity(code, lon, is_day=is_day, cfg=cfg),
            "speed": speed,
            "mean_speed": mean,
            "speed_diff": None if mean is None else compared - mean,
            "speed_percent": None if mean is None else (compared - mean) / mean * 100.0,
            "retrograde": speed < 0,
            "sect": sect,
            "in_sect": None if sect is None else (sect == "diurnal") == bool(is_day),
            "oriental": oriental,
            "solar_phase": solar_phase(code, lon, sun_lon, cfg.get("sun") or {}),
            "lat_dms": dms(float(p.get("lat") or 0.0), signed=True),
            "ra_dms": dms(norm360(p.get("ra") or 0.0)),
            "dec_dms": dms(float(p.get("dec") or 0.0), signed=True),
        })
    return rows


def point_rows(points: Mapping[str, Mapping[str, Any]], **extra_keys: str) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    """小行星、恒星、特殊点、宫头等：黄经、黄纬、赤经、赤纬（度分秒）和速度。"""
    rows = []
    for name, p in points.items():
        row = {
            "name": str(name),
            **position(p["lon"]),
            "speed": float(p.get("speed") or 0.0),
        }
        if "ra" in p:
            row["ra_dms"] = dms(norm360(p["ra"]))
        if "dec" in p:
            row["dec_dms"] = dms(float(p["dec"]), signed=True)
        if "lat" in p:
            row["lat_dms"] = dms(float(p["lat"]), signed=True)
        for out_key, src_key in extra_keys.items():
            if src_key in p:
                row[out_key] = p[src_key]
        rows.append(row)
    return rows


########## FILE: systems\western\astro\lots.py ##########
"""Lot（阿拉伯点）：公式解析与计算。

公式由若干项相加减组成，结果对 360° 取余，例如福点（日生）：``Asc + Mo - Su``。
每个 Lot 有自己的日夜规则 sect_mode（auto / day / night），见 choose_formula。

每一项可以是：
  - 行星 / 交点：Su Mo Me Ve Ma Ju Sa Ur Ne Pl Ra Ke
  - 四轴：Asc MC Desc IC
  - 宫头：H1 … H12
  - 特殊点：MeanApog MeanPerig TrueApog TruePerig IntpApog IntpPerig Syzygy Vertex AntiVertex EastPoint
  - 小行星：#编号，例如 #3（婚神星）、#433（爱神星）
  - 恒星：*{名称}，例如 *{Regulus}、*{,alLeo}
  - 另一个 Lot：@代码，例如 @Fortune
  - 常数度数：例如 30 或 30°
代码不区分大小写。公式里引用的点即使在设置里没有启用，也会单独算出来参与计算。

本模块只做公式解析和求值，不直接调用 Swiss Ephemeris：位置由调用方传入的
resolver(kind, value) 提供（见 systems/western/bridge.py 的 PointResolver）。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import re
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Callable

PLANET_CODES = ("Su", "Mo", "Me", "Ve", "Ma", "Ju", "Sa", "Ur", "Ne", "Pl", "Ra", "Ke")
AXIS_CODES = ("Asc", "MC", "Desc", "IC")
CUSP_CODES = tuple(f"H{n}" for n in range(1, 13))
SPECIAL_CODES = (
    "MeanApog", "MeanPerig", "TrueApog", "TruePerig", "IntpApog", "IntpPerig",
    "Syzygy", "Vertex", "AntiVertex", "EastPoint",
)

_NAMED = {code.casefold(): (kind, code) for kind, codes in (
    ("planet", PLANET_CODES), ("axis", AXIS_CODES), ("cusp", CUSP_CODES), ("special", SPECIAL_CODES),
) for code in codes}

LOT_CODE_RE = re.compile(r"^[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9_]{0,19}$")
MAX_FORMULA_LEN = 300
MAX_TERMS = 20

_TOKEN_RE = re.compile(
    r"""\s*(?:
        (?P<op>[+\-])
      | (?P<num>\d+(?:\.\d+)?)\s*°?
      | \*\{(?P<star>[^{}]+)\}
      | \#(?P<ast>\d+)
      | @(?P<lot>[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9_]*)
      | (?P<name>[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9]*)
    )""",
    re.VERBOSE,
)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Term:
    sign: int            # +1 或 -1
    kind: str            # planet / axis / cusp / special / asteroid / star / lot / const
    value: object        # 代码、编号、恒星查询串、Lot 代码或常数

    def text(self) -> str:
        if self.kind == "asteroid":
            return f"#{self.value}"
        if self.kind == "star":
            return f"*{{{self.value}}}"
        if self.kind == "lot":
            return f"@{self.value}"
        if self.kind == "const":
            return f"{self.value:g}"
        return str(self.value)


def parse_formula(text: str) -> list[Term]:
    """把公式文本解析成项的列表；写法不对时抛 ValueError（信息可以直接显示给用户）。"""
    source = " ".join(str(text or "").split())
    if not source:
        raise ValueError("公式不能为空。")
    if len(source) > MAX_FORMULA_LEN:
        raise ValueError(f"公式不能超过 {MAX_FORMULA_LEN} 个字符。")
    source = source.replace("＋", "+").replace("－", "-").replace("−", "-")

    terms: list[Term] = []
    pos = 0
    sign = None              # 当前项前面的符号；None 表示还没读到运算符
    expect_term = True
    while pos < len(source):
        m = _TOKEN_RE.match(source, pos)
        if not m or m.end() == pos:
            raise ValueError(f"公式在「{source[pos:pos + 12]}」处无法识别。")
        pos = m.end()
        if m.group("op"):
            if not expect_term and sign is None:
                sign = 1 if m.group("op") == "+" else -1
                expect_term = True
                continue
            if expect_term and sign is None and not terms:
                sign = 1 if m.group("op") == "+" else -1    # 开头的正负号
                continue
            raise ValueError("公式里有连续的运算符。")
        if not expect_term:
            raise ValueError("两项之间缺少 + 或 -。")
        s = sign if sign is not None else 1
        if m.group("num"):
            terms.append(Term(s, "const", float(m.group("num"))))
        elif m.group("star"):
            query = " ".join(m.group("star").split())
            if not query:
                raise ValueError("恒星名称不能为空：*{名称}。")
            terms.append(Term(s, "star", query))
        elif m.group("ast"):
            number = int(m.group("ast"))
            if number < 1:
                raise ValueError("小行星编号必须大于 0。")
            terms.append(Term(s, "asteroid", number))
        elif m.group("lot"):
            terms.append(Term(s, "lot", m.group("lot")))
        else:
            name = m.group("name")
            found = _NAMED.get(name.casefold())
            if found is None:
                raise ValueError(
                    f"不认识「{name}」。行星写 Su、Mo…，四轴写 Asc、MC、Desc、IC，宫头写 H1–H12，"
                    "小行星写 #编号，恒星写 *{名称}，其它 Lot 写 @代码。"
                )
            terms.append(Term(s, found[0], found[1]))
        sign = None
        expect_term = False
        if len(terms) > MAX_TERMS:
            raise ValueError(f"公式最多 {MAX_TERMS} 项。")
    if expect_term:
        raise ValueError("公式不能以运算符结尾。")
    return terms


def normalize_formula(text: str) -> str:
    """解析后重新写成规范的形式（统一大小写和空格），方便显示和保存。"""
    terms = parse_formula(text)
    out = []
    for i, term in enumerate(terms):
        op = "-" if term.sign < 0 else "+"
        if i == 0:
            out.append(("-" if term.sign < 0 else "") + term.text())
        else:
            out.append(f"{op} {term.text()}")
    return " ".join(out)


#: resolver(kind, value) → (黄经, 每日速度)。速度用来判断 Lot 参与相位时是入相还是出相。
Resolver = Callable[[str, object], tuple[float, float]]

SECT_MODES = ("auto", "day", "night")


def evaluate(terms: list[Term], resolve: Resolver) -> tuple[float, float]:
    """按公式求 (黄经, 速度)：黄经对 360° 取余；速度就是各项速度按同样的正负号相加（常数项速度为 0）。"""
    total = 0.0
    speed = 0.0
    for term in terms:
        if term.kind == "const":
            total += term.sign * float(term.value)
            continue
        lon, spd = resolve(term.kind, term.value)
        total += term.sign * float(lon)
        speed += term.sign * float(spd)
    return total % 360.0, speed


def lot_sect_mode(lot: dict) -> str:
    mode = str(lot.get("sect_mode") or "auto")
    return mode if mode in SECT_MODES else "auto"


def choose_formula(lot: dict, *, is_day: bool | None) -> tuple[str, str]:
    """按这个 Lot 自己的日夜规则选公式：返回 (公式文本, "day"/"night"/"single")。

    lot["sect_mode"]：auto（按出生时太阳是否在地平线上）、day（固定日间公式）、night（固定夜间公式）。
    没有填写夜间公式的 Lot 不分日夜，始终用同一条公式。
    auto 模式下需要传入 is_day；其它情况 is_day 可以是 None。
    """
    night = str(lot.get("night") or "").strip()
    if not night:
        return str(lot["day"]), "single"
    mode = lot_sect_mode(lot)
    if mode == "auto":
        if is_day is None:
            raise ValueError("自动判断日夜时需要出生时的日夜信息。")
        use_day = is_day
    else:
        use_day = mode == "day"
    return (str(lot["day"]), "day") if use_day else (night, "night")


def needs_sect(lot: dict) -> bool:
    """这个 Lot 是否需要知道出生时是白天还是夜晚。"""
    return bool(str(lot.get("night") or "").strip()) and lot_sect_mode(lot) == "auto"


########## FILE: systems\western\astro\points.py ##########
"""小行星、恒星和特殊点（月亮远/近地点、朔望点、Vertex、东方点）。

所有位置都通过 Swiss Ephemeris 的星历文件计算（FLG_SWIEPH），不使用 Moshier 近似算法：
- 小行星：swe.calc_ut。谷神、智神、婚神、灶神、凯龙、人龙在主小行星文件 seas_*.se1 里；
  其它编号的小行星在各自的文件里（例如 433 爱神星 = ast0/se00433s.se1），文件不在时
  Swiss Ephemeris 直接报错（小行星没有近似算法可以回退），这里把错误逐条返回，不影响其它点。
- 恒星：swe.fixstar2_ut，读取星历目录里的 sefstars.txt。
- 月亮远/近地点：swe.calc_ut 的 MEAN_APOG / OSCU_APOG / INTP_APOG / INTP_PERG。
- 朔望点：用 swe.calc_ut 的日月位置与速度，牛顿迭代求出生前最近一次朔或望的时刻。
- Vertex / AntiVertex / EastPoint：直接取自 core.calculate_houses 的 ascmc。

每个函数都在 _SWISS_LOCK 里自行恢复星历路径与岁差状态，可以单独调用。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import re
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Iterable, Mapping

import swisseph as swe

from common.astro.core import (
    AstroContext,
    _SWISS_LOCK,
    _calc_body,
    _check_ephemeris_result,
    _jd_to_local,
    _position_flags,
    set_ephemeris_path,
)

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- 小行星

#: 这几个小行星在 Swiss Ephemeris 里有专用编号，数据在 seas_*.se1 里，
#: 不需要单独的 ast*/se*.se1 文件。用户输入它们的 MPC 编号时自动换成专用编号。
BUILTIN_ASTEROIDS: dict[int, int] = {
    1: swe.CERES,
    2: swe.PALLAS,
    3: swe.JUNO,
    4: swe.VESTA,
    2060: swe.CHIRON,
    5145: swe.PHOLUS,
}

MAX_ASTEROID_NUMBER = 9_999_999


def asteroid_body_id(number: int) -> int:
    """MPC 小行星编号 → Swiss Ephemeris 的星体编号。"""
    number = int(number)
    if not 1 <= number <= MAX_ASTEROID_NUMBER:
        raise ValueError(f"小行星编号必须在 1–{MAX_ASTEROID_NUMBER} 之间。")
    return BUILTIN_ASTEROIDS.get(number, swe.AST_OFFSET + number)


def asteroid_file_hint(number: int) -> str:
    """这颗小行星的星历文件应放在星历目录下的哪个位置（主小行星文件里的返回空串）。"""
    number = int(number)
    if number in BUILTIN_ASTEROIDS:
        return ""
    name = f"s{number:06d}s.se1" if number > 99999 else f"se{number:05d}s.se1"
    return f"ast{number // 1000}/{name}"


def _friendly_error(exc: Exception, number: int | None = None) -> str:
    text = str(exc)
    text = re.sub(r"^swisseph\.\w+:\s*", "", text)
    if number is not None and "not found" in text:
        hint = asteroid_file_hint(number)
        if hint:
            return f"缺少星历文件：请把 {hint.split('/')[-1]} 放到 common/astro/ephe/{hint.rsplit('/', 1)[0]}/ 里（{text}）"
    return text


def calculate_asteroids(
    context: AstroContext,
    items: Iterable[Mapping[str, Any]],
    *,
    ecliptic_mode: str,
    ayanamsha_mode: str | int,
) -> tuple[dict[str, dict[str, Any]], list[dict[str, str]]]:
    """计算一组小行星。items 的每一项至少有 number，可带 label。

    返回 (结果, 错误列表)。结果的键是页面上显示的名字；某一颗算不出来时
    只把它放进错误列表，其余照常返回。
    """
    results: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
    errors: list[dict[str, str]] = []
    with _SWISS_LOCK:
        set_ephemeris_path(context.ephe_path)
        flags = _position_flags(ecliptic_mode, ayanamsha_mode)
        for item in items:
            number = int(item["number"])
            label = str(item.get("label") or "").strip()
            key = f"{label} #{number}" if label and label != f"#{number}" else f"#{number}"
            try:
                data = _calc_body(context.jd_utc, asteroid_body_id(number), flags, key)
            except (swe.Error, FileNotFoundError, ValueError) as exc:
                errors.append({"label": label or f"#{number}", "error": _friendly_error(exc, number)})
                continue
            results[key] = {**data, "number": number}
    return results, errors


def check_asteroid(number: int, *, ephe_path: str | Path | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """新增小行星前检查：Swiss Ephemeris 里的名字，以及星历文件是否可用。

    文件不在时 ok=False 并给出应放置的位置（仍然允许添加，放好文件后即可计算）。
    """
    body = asteroid_body_id(number)
    result: dict[str, Any] = {"number": int(number), "name": "", "ok": True, "error": "",
                              "file": asteroid_file_hint(number)}
    with _SWISS_LOCK:
        set_ephemeris_path(ephe_path)
        jd = swe.julday(2000, 1, 1, 12.0)
        try:
            xx, ret_flag, serr = _unpack_calc(swe.calc_ut(jd, body, swe.FLG_SWIEPH))
            _check_ephemeris_result(ret_flag, swe.FLG_SWIEPH, f"#{number}", serr=serr)
        except (swe.Error, FileNotFoundError) as exc:
            result["ok"] = False
            result["error"] = _friendly_error(exc, int(number))
        try:
            result["name"] = str(swe.get_planet_name(body) or "").strip()
        except swe.Error:
            pass
    return result


def _unpack_calc(raw):
    xx = raw[0]
    ret_flag = raw[1] if len(raw) > 1 and isinstance(raw[1], int) else None
    serr = raw[2] if len(raw) > 2 and isinstance(raw[2], str) else ""
    return xx, ret_flag, serr


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- 恒星

def _star_query_ok(query: str) -> str:
    query = " ".join(str(query or "").split())
    if not query:
        raise ValueError("恒星名称不能为空。")
    # Swiss Ephemeris 把纯数字当作 sefstars.txt 里的“第几颗星”，容易误用，这里不接受。
    if query.isdigit():
        raise ValueError("请输入恒星名称（如 Regulus）或命名（如 ,alLeo），不要只输入数字。")
    return query


def check_star(query: str, *, ephe_path: str | Path | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """在 sefstars.txt 里查找恒星，返回规范的查询串、文件里的名字和星等。找不到时报错。"""
    query = _star_query_ok(query)
    with _SWISS_LOCK:
        set_ephemeris_path(ephe_path)
        try:
            mag, stnam = swe.fixstar2_mag(query)
        except swe.Error as exc:
            raise ValueError(f"在 sefstars.txt 里找不到恒星「{query}」：{_friendly_error(exc)}") from exc
    traditional, _, nomenclature = str(stnam).partition(",")
    # 以命名（如 ,alLeo）作为保存的查询串：同一颗星的多个传统名都指向同一行。
    canonical = f",{nomenclature.strip()}" if nomenclature.strip() else traditional.strip()
    return {
        "query": canonical,
        "name": traditional.strip(),
        "nomenclature": nomenclature.strip(),
        "magnitude": float(mag),
    }


def calculate_fixed_stars(
    context: AstroContext,
    items: Iterable[Mapping[str, Any]],
    *,
    ecliptic_mode: str,
    ayanamsha_mode: str | int,
) -> tuple[dict[str, dict[str, Any]], list[dict[str, str]]]:
    """计算一组恒星（含岁差、自行、章动、光行差）。items 的每一项有 query，可带 label。"""
    results: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
    errors: list[dict[str, str]] = []
    with _SWISS_LOCK:
        set_ephemeris_path(context.ephe_path)
        flags = _position_flags(ecliptic_mode, ayanamsha_mode)
        eq_flags = (flags & ~swe.FLG_SIDEREAL) | swe.FLG_EQUATORIAL
        for item in items:
            query = str(item.get("query") or "")
            label = str(item.get("label") or "").strip() or query
            try:
                data = star_position(context.jd_utc, query, flags, eq_flags)
            except (swe.Error, FileNotFoundError, ValueError) as exc:
                errors.append({"label": label, "error": _friendly_error(exc)})
                continue
            results[label] = data
    return results, errors


def star_position(jd_utc: float, query: str, flags: int, eq_flags: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """单颗恒星的黄道 + 赤道坐标（调用方负责加锁、设置星历路径和岁差）。"""
    query = _star_query_ok(query)
    xx, stnam, ret_flag = swe.fixstar2_ut(query, jd_utc, flags)
    _check_ephemeris_result(ret_flag, flags, f"恒星 {query}")
    eq, _, _ = swe.fixstar2_ut(query, jd_utc, eq_flags)
    mag, _ = swe.fixstar2_mag(query)
    return {
        "lon": float(xx[0] % 360.0),
        "lat": float(xx[1]),
        "speed": float(xx[3]),        # 岁差与自行带来的每日黄经变化，非常小；判断入相 / 出相时用
        "ra": float(eq[0] % 360.0),
        "dec": float(eq[1]),
        "mag": float(mag),
        "star": str(stnam),
        "bayer": bayer_name(str(stnam).partition(",")[2]),
    }


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- 特殊点

#: 代码 → (名称, 说明)。代码同时用在 Lot 公式里。
SPECIAL_POINTS: dict[str, tuple[str, str]] = {
    "MeanApog": ("平均远月点", "黑月莉莉丝 Mean Lilith：月球平均轨道的远地点"),
    "MeanPerig": ("平均近月点", "平均远月点的对冲点（Mean Priapus）"),
    "TrueApog": ("真实远月点", "密切轨道远地点（Osculating / True Lilith）"),
    "TruePerig": ("真实近月点", "密切轨道近地点，即真实远月点的对冲点"),
    "IntpApog": ("插值远月点", "Swiss Ephemeris 的插值远地点（Natural / Interpolated Apogee）"),
    "IntpPerig": ("插值近月点", "Swiss Ephemeris 的插值近地点（Natural / Interpolated Perigee）"),
    "Syzygy": ("朔望点", "出生前最近一次新月或满月时月亮所在的位置"),
    "Vertex": ("宿命点", "Vertex：卯酉圈与黄道在西方的交点"),
    "AntiVertex": ("反宿命点", "Vertex 的对冲点"),
    "EastPoint": ("东方点", "East Point：赤道上升点"),
}

_APSIS_BODIES = {
    "MeanApog": swe.MEAN_APOG,
    "TrueApog": swe.OSCU_APOG,
    "IntpApog": swe.INTP_APOG,
    "IntpPerig": swe.INTP_PERG,
}
# 平均 / 密切近地点与对应的远地点位于同一条拱线的两端，三维方向严格相反，
# 投影到黄道上也就是黄经 +180°、黄纬取反（与 swe.nod_aps_ut 的结果一致）。
_OPPOSITE_OF = {"MeanPerig": "MeanApog", "TruePerig": "TrueApog"}


def _opposite(point: Mapping[str, float]) -> dict[str, float]:
    return {
        "lon": (point["lon"] + 180.0) % 360.0,
        "lat": -point["lat"],
        "speed": point["speed"],
        "ra": (point["ra"] + 180.0) % 360.0,
        "dec": -point["dec"],
    }


def special_point(
    context: AstroContext,
    code: str,
    *,
    ecliptic_mode: str,
    ayanamsha_mode: str | int,
    house_result: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """计算一个特殊点。Vertex / AntiVertex / EastPoint 需要传入 core.calculate_houses 的结果。"""
    if code not in SPECIAL_POINTS:
        raise ValueError(f"未知的特殊点：{code}")
    if code in ("Vertex", "AntiVertex", "EastPoint"):
        if not house_result or code not in (house_result.get("extra") or {}):
            raise ValueError(f"{code} 需要宫位计算结果。")
        return dict(house_result["extra"][code])
    if code == "Syzygy":
        return prenatal_syzygy(context, ecliptic_mode=ecliptic_mode, ayanamsha_mode=ayanamsha_mode)
    with _SWISS_LOCK:
        set_ephemeris_path(context.ephe_path)
        flags = _position_flags(ecliptic_mode, ayanamsha_mode)
        if code in _OPPOSITE_OF:
            base = _calc_body(context.jd_utc, _APSIS_BODIES[_OPPOSITE_OF[code]], flags, code)
            return _opposite(base)
        return _calc_body(context.jd_utc, _APSIS_BODIES[code], flags, code)


def calculate_special_points(
    context: AstroContext,
    codes: Iterable[str],
    *,
    ecliptic_mode: str,
    ayanamsha_mode: str | int,
    house_result: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
) -> tuple[dict[str, dict[str, Any]], list[dict[str, str]]]:
    results: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
    errors: list[dict[str, str]] = []
    for code in codes:
        label = SPECIAL_POINTS[code][0]
        try:
            results[label] = {
                **special_point(
                    context, code, ecliptic_mode=ecliptic_mode,
                    ayanamsha_mode=ayanamsha_mode, house_result=house_result,
                ),
                "code": code,
            }
        except (swe.Error, FileNotFoundError, ValueError) as exc:
            errors.append({"label": label, "error": _friendly_error(exc)})
    return results, errors


def _elongation(jd: float) -> tuple[float, float]:
    """月亮减太阳的黄经差（0–360）及其每日变化率。只是差值，与黄道体系无关。"""
    flags = swe.FLG_SWIEPH | swe.FLG_SPEED
    sun, sflag, serr = _unpack_calc(swe.calc_ut(jd, swe.SUN, flags))
    _check_ephemeris_result(sflag, flags, "朔望点（太阳）", serr=serr)
    moon, mflag, merr = _unpack_calc(swe.calc_ut(jd, swe.MOON, flags))
    _check_ephemeris_result(mflag, flags, "朔望点（月亮）", serr=merr)
    return (moon[0] - sun[0]) % 360.0, moon[3] - sun[3]


def prenatal_syzygy(
    context: AstroContext,
    *,
    ecliptic_mode: str,
    ayanamsha_mode: str | int,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """出生前最近一次朔（新月）或望（满月），返回那一刻月亮的位置。

    新月时日月同度，取月亮即可；满月时取月亮的位置（不采用托勒密“取地平线上的发光体”的变体）。
    """
    with _SWISS_LOCK:
        set_ephemeris_path(context.ephe_path)
        elong, _ = _elongation(context.jd_utc)
        target = 0.0 if elong < 180.0 else 180.0
        kind = "新月" if target == 0.0 else "满月"
        # 出生时刻刚好是朔望：就是它本身；否则从平均月相速度估一个起点，再用牛顿法收敛。
        jd = context.jd_utc - (elong - target) / 12.190749
        for _ in range(30):
            e, rate = _elongation(jd)
            diff = (e - target + 180.0) % 360.0 - 180.0
            step = diff / rate
            jd -= step
            if abs(step) < 1e-9:
                break
        else:
            raise RuntimeError("朔望点迭代没有收敛。")
        if jd > context.jd_utc + 1e-9:
            raise RuntimeError("朔望点计算出现异常（结果晚于出生时刻）。")
        flags = _position_flags(ecliptic_mode, ayanamsha_mode)
        moon = _calc_body(jd, swe.MOON, flags, "朔望点")
    tz = context.local_dt.tzinfo
    return {
        **moon,
        # 朔望点是一个已经过去的时刻里月亮的位置，固定不动：速度为 0（判断入相 / 出相时只看行星的运动）。
        "speed": 0.0,
        "type": kind,
        "time": _jd_to_local(jd, tz).isoformat(timespec="seconds"),
    }


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- 日夜

def sun_above_horizon(context: AstroContext, rule: str = "geometric") -> bool:
    """出生时太阳是否在地平线以上：日生盘为 True。

    rule：geometric 太阳中心的几何高度 > 0（不计大气折射）；
          apparent  太阳中心的视高度 > 0（按标准大气 1013.25 hPa、15 °C 计入折射，约提前 / 推后 2–3 分钟）。
    """
    with _SWISS_LOCK:
        set_ephemeris_path(context.ephe_path)
        flags = swe.FLG_SWIEPH | swe.FLG_EQUATORIAL
        eq, ret_flag, serr = _unpack_calc(swe.calc_ut(context.jd_utc, swe.SUN, flags))
        _check_ephemeris_result(ret_flag, flags, "太阳（日夜判断）", serr=serr)
        _, true_alt, app_alt = swe.azalt(
            context.jd_utc, swe.EQU2HOR, (context.lon, context.lat, 0.0), 1013.25, 15.0,
            (eq[0], eq[1], eq[2]),
        )
    return float(app_alt if rule == "apparent" else true_alt) > 0.0


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- 恒星的拜耳命名

_GREEK = {
    "al": "α", "be": "β", "ga": "γ", "de": "δ", "ep": "ε", "ze": "ζ", "et": "η", "th": "θ",
    "io": "ι", "ka": "κ", "la": "λ", "mu": "μ", "nu": "ν", "xi": "ξ", "omi": "ο", "pi": "π",
    "rh": "ρ", "si": "σ", "ta": "τ", "up": "υ", "ph": "φ", "ch": "χ", "ps": "ψ", "ome": "ω",
}
_SUPERSCRIPT = str.maketrans("0123456789", "⁰¹²³⁴⁵⁶⁷⁸⁹")
_BAYER_RE = re.compile(r"^(ome|omi|al|be|ga|de|ep|ze|et|th|io|ka|la|mu|nu|xi|pi|rh|si|ta|up|ph|ch|ps)(?:-(\d))?([A-Z][A-Za-z]{2})$")


def bayer_name(nomenclature: str) -> str:
    """sefstars.txt 的命名 → 拜耳命名：bePer → β Per，al-2Lib → α² Lib；
    不是拜耳命名的（弗兰斯蒂德编号、梅西耶天体等）原样返回，如 61Cyg → 61 Cyg。"""
    text = str(nomenclature or "").strip()
    m = _BAYER_RE.match(text)
    if m:
        return f"{_GREEK[m.group(1)]}{(m.group(2) or '').translate(_SUPERSCRIPT)} {m.group(3)}"
    m = re.match(r"^(\d+)([A-Z][A-Za-z]{2})$", text)
    if m:
        return f"{m.group(1)} {m.group(2)}"
    return text


########## FILE: systems\western\astro\__init__.py ##########
"""西方占星专用的计算：小行星、恒星、特殊点（points）、Lot（lots）、相位（aspects）。

行星、宫位、星历路径等共用基础来自 common.astro。
"""

from .aspects import ORB_RULES, AspectPoint, AspectType, aspect_between, find_aspects
from .lots import choose_formula, evaluate, normalize_formula, parse_formula

    BUILTIN_ASTEROIDS,
    SPECIAL_POINTS,
    asteroid_body_id,
    calculate_asteroids,

    calculate_special_points,
    check_asteroid,
    check_star,
    prenatal_syzygy,

    sun_above_horizon,
)

__all__ = [
    "BUILTIN_ASTEROIDS", "ORB_RULES", "SPECIAL_POINTS", "AspectPoint", "AspectType", "aspect_between",
    "asteroid_body_id", "calculate_asteroids", "calculate_fixed_stars", "calculate_special_points",
    "check_asteroid", "check_star", "choose_formula", "evaluate", "find_aspects", "normalize_formula",
    "parse_formula", "prenatal_syzygy", "special_point", "sun_above_horizon",

########## FILE: systems\western\static\chart.js ##########
"use strict";
/*
 * 西方圆形星盘（纯前端 SVG 绘制，不依赖任何第三方库）。
 *
 * 用法：AstroChart.render(容器元素, 排盘结果, {emptyText, symbols, onError})
 *   symbols：{planets: {Su: "☉", …}, specials: {MeanApog: "⚸", …}}（来自 /api/bootstrap 的 options）
 *   onError(message)：盘中元素太多、排不下时调用（星盘仍画出框架，但不画盘中元素）。
 *
 * 13 个同心圆（从外往里编号 1–13）：
 *   1、3、5、11、13  画出来的边界圆
 *   2                宫头的星座度数。顺着逆时针方向依次是：分、星座符号、度；离得太近时自动挤开到刚好不挨着
 *   4                恒星：只画与行星构成相位的恒星，一律用星号，鼠标悬停看详情
 *   6–10             盘中元素（行星、小行星、特殊点、Lot）：符号、度、星座符号、分、逆行标志 R
 *   12               宫位数字
 *   宫位线           从第 13 圈画到第 5 圈
 *
 * 约定：
 * - 第 1 宫宫头永远在正左边（整宫制也一样），黄道度数沿逆时针增加。
 * - 所有文字水平正向显示，不随位置旋转。
 * - 一个盘中元素的 5 个子项排在同一条从圆心出发的射线上。两个元素的任何子项会重叠时，
 *   不单独挪动某个子项，而是调整射线之间的角度（见 spreadOnCircle）。挤不下时报“排盘错误”。
 * - 边界圆和宫位线画在最下层，盘中元素在最上层。第 5 圈内侧的短刻度标出每个元素的真实黄经。
 * - 颜色全部由 CSS 变量控制。
 *
 * 点击交互：点一个盘中元素 → 高亮它，以及与它构成相位的元素和恒星；再点一次或点空白处取消。
 */
(function () {
    // ------------------------------------------------------------ 可调参数

    const SIZE = 640;
    const C = SIZE / 2;

    // 13 个同心圆的半径（从外往里）。
    // 13 个同心圆的半径（从外往里，SVG 单位，整张盘 640×640）。
    // 第 1–5 圈（宫头星座度数带、恒星带）保持不变；第 11–13 圈（宫位数字带）尽量往里收，
    // 把空间让给第 6–10 圈的盘中元素。改这里的数字时，相邻两圈的间距要放得下各自的文字。
    const R = [null, 310, 292, 274, 262, 250, 224, 192, 163, 136, 112, 96, 85, 74];
    const VISIBLE_CIRCLES = [1, 3, 5, 11, 13];

    // 字号（SVG 单位，随星盘整体缩放）
    // 第 6–10 圈（盘中元素）从外往里逐级变小：符号 → 度 → 星座符号 → 分 → R，视觉重心落在符号上。
    // 星座符号按“看上去的大小”定字号（符号字体的字形比同字号的数字略小，所以字号数值比度数只小一点）。
    // 行星符号本身（symGlyph）的大小不变；星盘整体大小（SIZE）不变。
    const FONT = {
        cuspDeg: 15.5, cuspSign: 19, cuspMin: 13.5,
        star: 14,
        symGlyph: 25, symText: 15,
        deg: 17.5, sign: 17, min: 13.5, retro: 11.5,
        house: 11,
    };
    const SYM_TEXT_MAX_WIDTH = 31;     // 多个字母的符号（如 Spi、⚸T）超过这个宽度时缩小字号
    const GAP = 2.2;                   // 相邻文字之间至少留出的距离（SVG 单位）
    const TICK = 6;                    // 第 5 圈内侧真实位置刻度的长度

    const TEXT_FAMILY = '"Segoe UI", "Microsoft YaHei", "PingFang SC", system-ui, sans-serif';
    const SYMBOL_FAMILY = '"Segoe UI Symbol", "Noto Sans Symbols 2", "Noto Sans Symbols", "Apple Symbols", "DejaVu Sans", sans-serif';

    const SIGN_GLYPHS = ["♈", "♉", "♊", "♋", "♌", "♍", "♎", "♏", "♐", "♑", "♒", "♓"];
    const SIGN_NAMES = ["白羊", "金牛", "双子", "巨蟹", "狮子", "处女", "天秤", "天蝎", "射手", "摩羯", "水瓶", "双鱼"];
    const PLANET_NAMES = {
        Su: "太阳", Mo: "月亮", Me: "水星", Ve: "金星", Ma: "火星", Ju: "木星", Sa: "土星",
        Ur: "天王星", Ne: "海王星", Pl: "冥王星", Ra: "罗睺（北交点）", Ke: "计都（南交点）",
    };
    const PLANET_ORDER = ["Su", "Mo", "Me", "Ve", "Ma", "Ju", "Sa", "Ur", "Ne", "Pl", "Ra", "Ke"];
    const STAR_GLYPH = "✦";

    const SVG_NS = "http://www.w3.org/2000/svg";
    let selection = null;            // 当前选中的盘中元素 id
    let lastErrorResults = null;     // 同一份结果只弹一次排盘错误

    // ------------------------------------------------------------ 小工具

    function el(tag, attrs = {}, parent = null) {
        const node = document.createElementNS(SVG_NS, tag);
        for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(attrs)) {
            if (v !== undefined && v !== null) node.setAttribute(k, String(v));
        }
        if (parent) parent.appendChild(node);
        return node;
    }

    function norm360(x) {
        return ((Number(x) % 360) + 360) % 360;
    }

    // 占星符号后面加 U+FE0E，避免在部分系统上显示成彩色表情。
    function symText(text) {
        return String(text ?? "").replace(/[☀-➿⯐-⯿]/g, (c) => c + "︎");
    }

    function splitLon(lon) {
        const x = norm360(lon);
        let sign = Math.floor(x / 30);
        let totalMin = Math.round((x - sign * 30) * 60);
        if (totalMin >= 30 * 60) {          // 29°59.5′ 以上四舍五入进位到下一个星座
            totalMin -= 30 * 60;
            sign = (sign + 1) % 12;
        }
        return {sign, deg: Math.floor(totalMin / 60), min: totalMin % 60};
    }

    function lonText(lon) {
        const p = splitLon(lon);
        return `${SIGN_NAMES[p.sign]} ${p.deg}°${String(p.min).padStart(2, "0")}′`;
    }

    // 用 canvas 量文字宽度（与 SVG 用同一套字体），高度取字号。
    const measureCtx = document.createElement("canvas").getContext("2d");
    function textBox(text, size, family, weight = 400) {
        measureCtx.font = `${weight} ${size}px ${family}`;
        return {w: measureCtx.measureText(text).width, h: size * 1.05};
    }

    function rad(deg) {
        return deg * Math.PI / 180;
    }

    // 屏幕角度（数学方向：0° 向右，逆时针增加，y 轴向上）→ SVG 坐标。
    function polar(r, angle) {
        return [C + r * Math.cos(rad(angle)), C - r * Math.sin(rad(angle))];
    }

    // 文字框沿某个角度的切向 / 径向占用长度（文字永远水平，框不旋转）。
    function tangentialExtent(box, angle) {
        return box.w * Math.abs(Math.sin(rad(angle))) + box.h * Math.abs(Math.cos(rad(angle)));
    }

    // 两个文字框中心位于同一圆周（半径 r）上时，至少要相隔多少度才不重叠。
    function angularNeed(boxA, angleA, boxB, angleB, r) {
        const sep = tangentialExtent(boxA, angleA) / 2 + tangentialExtent(boxB, angleB) / 2 + GAP;
        return 2 * Math.asin(Math.min(1, sep / (2 * r))) * 180 / Math.PI;
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------ 圆周上的避让排布
    //
    // items：[{angle（真实位置，度）}]，按逆时针排好序。needFn(a, b, angleA, angleB)：a、b 相邻时至少相隔多少度。
    // 返回每一项的显示角度：在不重叠的前提下尽量贴近真实位置（相邻的挤成一团时，整团的平均偏移为 0）。
    // 一圈放不下时抛出错误。

    function spreadOnCircle(items, needFn) {
        const n = items.length;
        if (n === 0) return [];
        if (n === 1) return [items[0].angle];
        let angles = items.map((it) => it.angle);
        for (let pass = 0; pass < 4; pass++) {
            const needs = items.map((it, i) => {
                const j = (i + 1) % n;
                return needFn(items[i], items[j], angles[i], angles[j]);
            });
            const total = needs.reduce((s, x) => s + x, 0);
            if (total > 360) {
                const error = new Error("overflow");
                error.overflow = true;
                throw error;
            }
            // 在真实位置之间最大的空隙处把圆周剪开，变成一条直线上的问题。
            let cut = 0;
            let best = -1;
            for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
                const gap = norm360(items[(i + 1) % n].angle - items[i].angle) || (n > 1 ? 0 : 360);
                if (gap > best) { best = gap; cut = (i + 1) % n; }
            }
            const order = [];
            for (let k = 0; k < n; k++) order.push((cut + k) % n);
            const t = [];
            let prev = null;
            for (const idx of order) {
                let a = items[idx].angle;
                if (prev !== null) while (a < prev) a += 360;
                t.push(a);
                prev = a;
            }
            // 相邻“团”合并：团内按最小间距排开，团的位置取成员真实位置的平均。
            const clusters = [];
            for (let k = 0; k < n; k++) {
                let cur = {members: [k], offsets: [0], pos: t[k]};
                while (clusters.length) {
                    const last = clusters[clusters.length - 1];
                    const lastK = last.members[last.members.length - 1];
                    const need = needs[order[lastK]];
                    const lastOff = last.offsets[last.offsets.length - 1];
                    if (last.pos + lastOff + need <= cur.pos + 1e-9) break;
                    clusters.pop();
                    const shift = lastOff + need;
                    const members = last.members.concat(cur.members);
                    const offsets = last.offsets.concat(cur.offsets.map((o) => o + shift));
                    const pos = members.reduce((s, m, i) => s + t[m] - offsets[i], 0) / members.length;
                    cur = {members, offsets, pos};
                }
                clusters.push(cur);
            }
            const linear = new Array(n);
            for (const c of clusters) c.members.forEach((m, i) => { linear[m] = c.pos + c.offsets[i]; });
            // 首尾跨过剪开处也不能重叠；不满足时整圈当作一团。
            const wrapNeed = needs[order[n - 1]];
            if (linear[n - 1] + wrapNeed > linear[0] + 360 + 1e-9) {
                const offsets = [0];
                for (let k = 1; k < n; k++) offsets.push(offsets[k - 1] + needs[order[k - 1]]);
                const pos = t.reduce((s, x, k) => s + x - offsets[k], 0) / n;
                for (let k = 0; k < n; k++) linear[k] = pos + offsets[k];
            }
            const next = new Array(n);
            order.forEach((idx, k) => { next[idx] = norm360(linear[k]); });
            angles = next;
        }
        return angles;
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------ 数据整理

    function aspectIndex(results) {
        const index = new Map();
        const labels = new Map();
        const add = (id, other, hit) => {
            if (!index.has(id)) index.set(id, []);
            index.get(id).push({other, hit});
        };
        const aspects = results.aspects || {};
        for (const p of aspects.main?.points || []) labels.set(p.id, p);
        for (const g of aspects.groups || []) for (const p of g.points) labels.set(p.id, p);
        for (const hit of aspects.main?.aspects || []) {
            add(hit.a, hit.b, hit);
            add(hit.b, hit.a, hit);
        }
        for (const g of aspects.groups || []) {
            if (g.key === "antiscia") continue;
            for (const hit of g.aspects) {
                add(hit.a, hit.b, hit);
                add(hit.b, hit.a, hit);
            }
        }
        return {index, labels};
    }

    function buildElements(results, symbols) {
        const items = [];
        const points = results.points || {};
        const pos = results.planet_pos || {};
        for (const code of PLANET_ORDER) {
            if (!pos[code]) continue;
            items.push({id: code, kind: "planet", symbol: symbols.planets?.[code] || code,
                color: points.planet_colors?.[code], name: PLANET_NAMES[code] || code, ...pos[code]});
        }
        const asteroidMeta = new Map((points.asteroids || []).map((a) => [a.number, a]));
        for (const [label, p] of Object.entries(results.asteroids || {})) {
            const meta = asteroidMeta.get(p.number);
            items.push({id: `#${p.number}`, kind: "asteroid", symbol: meta?.symbol || `#${p.number}`, color: meta?.color,
                name: meta ? `${meta.label} #${p.number}` : label, ...p});
        }
        for (const [label, p] of Object.entries(results.special_points || {})) {
            const style = points.special_styles?.[p.code];
            items.push({id: `sp:${p.code}`, kind: "special", symbol: style?.symbol || symbols.specials?.[p.code] || p.code.slice(0, 3),
                color: style?.color, name: label, ...p});
        }
        const lotMeta = new Map((points.lots?.items || []).map((l) => [l.label, l]));
        for (const [label, p] of Object.entries(results.lots || {})) {
            items.push({id: `lot:${label}`, kind: "lot", symbol: lotMeta.get(label)?.symbol || label.slice(0, 3),
                color: lotMeta.get(label)?.color, name: label, ...p});
        }
        return items;
    }

    // 只画与行星构成相位的恒星。
    function activeStars(results) {
        const group = (results.aspects?.groups || []).find((g) => g.key === "stars");
        if (!group) return [];
        const active = new Set(group.aspects.map((h) => h.a));
        const meta = new Map((results.points?.stars || []).map((s) => [s.label, s]));
        return Object.entries(results.fixed_stars || {})
            .filter(([label]) => active.has(`st:${label}`))
            .map(([label, p]) => ({id: `st:${label}`, name: label, color: meta.get(label)?.color, ...p}));
    }

    function cuspList(results) {
        const houses = results.house_result?.houses || {};
        const list = [];
        for (let i = 1; i <= 12; i++) {
            const h = houses[`house ${i}`];
            if (!h) return null;
            list.push(Number(h.lon));
        }
        return list;
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------ 各层排版

    // 盘中元素：每个元素 5 个子项（符号、度、星座、分、R），各自的文字框。
    // 星座符号的颜色（设置里的星座配色）；没有设置时用 CSS 的默认颜色。
    let signColors = [];
    let retroColor = "";               // 逆行标志 R 的颜色（星体配置 → 行星）
    function fillStyle(color) {
        return color ? `fill:${color}` : undefined;
    }

    function elementParts(item) {
        const p = splitLon(item.lon);
        const glyphOnly = /^[☀-➿⯐-⯿][′']?$/.test(item.symbol);
        let symSize = glyphOnly ? FONT.symGlyph : FONT.symText;
        let symBox = textBox(symText(item.symbol), symSize, SYMBOL_FAMILY, glyphOnly ? 400 : 700);
        if (!glyphOnly && symBox.w > SYM_TEXT_MAX_WIDTH) {
            symSize = symSize * SYM_TEXT_MAX_WIDTH / symBox.w;
            symBox = textBox(symText(item.symbol), symSize, SYMBOL_FAMILY, 700);
        }
        const retro = Number(item.speed) < 0;
        const parts = [
            {ring: 6, cls: "el-sym", text: symText(item.symbol), size: symSize, family: SYMBOL_FAMILY,
                weight: glyphOnly ? 400 : 700, box: symBox, style: fillStyle(item.color)},
            {ring: 7, cls: "el-deg", text: `${p.deg}°`, size: FONT.deg, family: TEXT_FAMILY, weight: 600},
            {ring: 8, cls: "el-sign", text: symText(SIGN_GLYPHS[p.sign]),
                size: FONT.sign, family: SYMBOL_FAMILY, weight: 400, style: fillStyle(signColors[p.sign])},
            {ring: 9, cls: "el-min", text: `${String(p.min).padStart(2, "0")}'`, size: FONT.min, family: TEXT_FAMILY, weight: 400},
        ];
        if (retro) parts.push({ring: 10, cls: "el-retro", text: "R", size: FONT.retro, family: TEXT_FAMILY, weight: 700,
            style: fillStyle(retroColor)});
        for (const part of parts) part.box = part.box || textBox(part.text, part.size, part.family, part.weight);
        return parts;
    }

    // 两个文字框（各自在半径 ra、rb 的圆上，角度 aa、ab）在屏幕上是否重叠（含间隙）。
    function boxesOverlap(boxA, ra, aa, boxB, rb, ab) {
        const [xa, ya] = polar(ra, aa);
        const [xb, yb] = polar(rb, ab);
        return Math.abs(xa - xb) < (boxA.w + boxB.w) / 2 + GAP && Math.abs(ya - yb) < (boxA.h + boxB.h) / 2 + GAP;
    }

    // 相邻两个元素至少要相隔多少度：同一圈的子项，以及相邻两圈的子项（斜对角）都不能重叠。
    // 以两者当前的中间角度为中心对称张开，二分查找刚好不重叠的角度。
    function elementNeed(a, b, angleA, angleB) {
        const mid = angleA + norm360(angleB - angleA) / 2;
        let need = 0;
        for (const pa of a.parts) {
            for (const pb of b.parts) {
                if (Math.abs(pa.ring - pb.ring) > 1) continue;
                const clash = (d) => boxesOverlap(pa.box, R[pa.ring], mid - d / 2, pb.box, R[pb.ring], mid + d / 2);
                if (!clash(need)) continue;
                let lo = need;
                let hi = 90;
                if (clash(hi)) { need = hi; continue; }
                for (let i = 0; i < 22; i++) {
                    const m = (lo + hi) / 2;
                    if (clash(m)) lo = m; else hi = m;
                }
                need = hi;
            }
        }
        return need;
    }

    // 宫头标签：顺着逆时针依次是 分、星座、度。
    function cuspLabelParts(lon) {
        const p = splitLon(lon);
        const parts = [
            {cls: "cusp-min", text: `${String(p.min).padStart(2, "0")}'`, size: FONT.cuspMin, family: TEXT_FAMILY, weight: 400},
            {cls: "cusp-sign", text: symText(SIGN_GLYPHS[p.sign]), size: FONT.cuspSign,
                family: SYMBOL_FAMILY, weight: 400, style: fillStyle(signColors[p.sign])},
            {cls: "cusp-deg", text: `${p.deg}°`, size: FONT.cuspDeg, family: TEXT_FAMILY, weight: 700},
        ];
        for (const part of parts) part.box = textBox(part.text, part.size, part.family, part.weight);
        return parts;
    }

    function cuspLabelLength(parts, angle) {
        return parts.reduce((s, part) => s + tangentialExtent(part.box, angle), 0) + GAP * (parts.length - 1);
    }

    function cuspNeed(a, b, angleA, angleB) {
        const half = (cuspLabelLength(a.parts, angleA) + cuspLabelLength(b.parts, angleB)) / 2 + GAP * 2;
        return 2 * Math.asin(Math.min(1, half / (2 * R[2]))) * 180 / Math.PI;
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------ 绘制

    function drawText(parent, x, y, part, extra = {}) {
        const node = el("text", {
            x: x.toFixed(2), y: y.toFixed(2),
            "text-anchor": "middle", "dominant-baseline": "central",
            "font-size": part.size.toFixed(2), "font-family": part.family, "font-weight": part.weight,
            class: part.cls, style: part.style, ...extra,
        }, parent);
        node.textContent = part.text;
        return node;
    }

    function tooltipFor(item, aspects) {
        const lines = [`${item.symbol} ${item.name}`, `${lonText(item.lon)}${Number(item.speed) < 0 ? "  逆行" : ""}`,
            `黄经 ${norm360(item.lon).toFixed(4)}°`];
        if (item.speed !== undefined) lines.push(`速度 ${Number(item.speed).toFixed(4)}°/日`);
        if (item.mag !== undefined) lines.push(`星等 ${item.mag}`);
        const list = aspects.index.get(item.id) || [];
        if (list.length) {
            lines.push("相位：");
            for (const {other, hit} of list) {
                const o = aspects.labels.get(other);
                const dev = Math.round(Math.abs(hit.deviation) * 60);
                lines.push(`  ${hit.symbol} ${hit.name} ${o ? `${o.symbol} ${o.label}` : other}  `
                    + `${Math.floor(dev / 60)}°${String(dev % 60).padStart(2, "0")}′${hit.motion || ""}`);
            }
        }
        return lines.join("\n");
    }

    function sectorPath(r1, r2, a1, a2) {
        const [x1, y1] = polar(r2, a1);
        const [x2, y2] = polar(r2, a2);
        const [x3, y3] = polar(r1, a2);
        const [x4, y4] = polar(r1, a1);
        const large = norm360(a2 - a1) > 180 ? 1 : 0;
        return `M${x1},${y1} A${r2},${r2} 0 ${large} 0 ${x2},${y2} L${x3},${y3} A${r1},${r1} 0 ${large} 1 ${x4},${y4} Z`;
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------ 恒星信息弹窗（点击恒星后出现）

    let starPopup = null;

    function closeStarPopup() {
        starPopup?.remove();
        starPopup = null;
    }

    document.addEventListener("mousedown", (event) => {
        if (starPopup && !starPopup.contains(event.target) && !event.target.closest?.(".chart-star")) closeStarPopup();
    });
    document.addEventListener("keydown", (event) => {
        if (event.key === "Escape") closeStarPopup();
    });

    function openStarPopup(container, anchor, star, aspects) {
        closeStarPopup();
        const pop = document.createElement("div");
        pop.className = "chart-pop";
        const esc = (t) => String(t).replace(/[&<>"']/g, (c) => ({"&": "&amp;", "<": "&lt;", ">": "&gt;", '"': "&quot;", "'": "&#39;"}[c]));
        const list = aspects.index.get(star.id) || [];
        const rows = list.map(({other, hit}) => {
            const o = aspects.labels.get(other);
            const dev = Math.round(Math.abs(hit.deviation) * 60);
            const color = o?.color ? ` style="color:${esc(o.color)}"` : "";
            return `<li><span class="pop-sym">${esc(symText(hit.symbol))}</span> ${esc(hit.name)} `
                + `<span class="pop-sym"${color}>${esc(symText(o?.symbol || ""))}</span> ${esc(o?.label || other)}`
                + `<span class="pop-dev motion-${hit.motion || "none"}">${Math.floor(dev / 60)}°${String(dev % 60).padStart(2, "0")}′${hit.motion || ""}</span></li>`;
        }).join("");
        const starColor = star.color ? ` style="color:${esc(star.color)}"` : "";
        pop.innerHTML = `
            <div class="pop-head"><span class="pop-title"><span class="pop-sym"${starColor}>${esc(symText(STAR_GLYPH))}</span> ${esc(star.name)}</span>
                <button type="button" class="icon-btn small pop-close" aria-label="关闭">×</button></div>
            <div class="pop-body">
                <div class="pop-row"><span>位置</span><b>${esc(lonText(star.lon))}</b></div>
                <div class="pop-row"><span>黄经</span><b>${norm360(star.lon).toFixed(4)}°</b></div>
                <div class="pop-row"><span>黄纬</span><b>${Number(star.lat ?? 0).toFixed(4)}°</b></div>
                ${star.mag !== undefined ? `<div class="pop-row"><span>星等</span><b>${esc(star.mag)}</b></div>` : ""}
                ${star.star ? `<div class="pop-row"><span>星表名</span><b>${esc(star.star)}</b></div>` : ""}
                <div class="pop-sub">相位</div>
                <ul class="pop-list">${rows || "<li class='muted'>无</li>"}</ul>
            </div>`;
        pop.querySelector(".pop-close").addEventListener("click", closeStarPopup);
        container.appendChild(pop);
        // 放在恒星旁边，不超出星盘范围。
        const box = container.getBoundingClientRect();
        const a = anchor.getBoundingClientRect();
        const w = pop.offsetWidth;
        const h = pop.offsetHeight;
        let left = a.left - box.left + a.width + 8;
        if (left + w > box.width) left = a.left - box.left - w - 8;
        let top = a.top - box.top - h / 2 + a.height / 2;
        left = Math.max(0, Math.min(left, box.width - w));
        top = Math.max(0, Math.min(top, box.height - h));
        pop.style.left = `${left}px`;
        pop.style.top = `${top}px`;
        starPopup = pop;
    }

    function render(container, results, options = {}) {
        closeStarPopup();
        container.innerHTML = "";
        const cusps = results ? cuspList(results) : null;
        if (!results || !results.planet_pos || !cusps) {
            const p = document.createElement("p");
            p.className = "chart-empty";
            p.textContent = options.emptyText || "排盘后显示星盘";
            container.appendChild(p);
            return null;
        }
        const symbols = options.symbols || {};
        signColors = results.points?.sign_colors || [];
        retroColor = results.points?.retro_color || "";
        const aspects = aspectIndex(results);
        const elements = buildElements(results, symbols);
        const stars = activeStars(results);
        if (selection && !elements.some((e) => e.id === selection)) selection = null;

        // 第 1 宫宫头在正左边（180°），黄经沿逆时针增加。
        const rotation = 180 - cusps[0];
        const screen = (lon) => norm360(Number(lon) + rotation);

        const svg = el("svg", {
            class: "astro-chart round", xmlns: SVG_NS, role: "img", "aria-label": "星盘",
            viewBox: `0 0 ${SIZE} ${SIZE}`, "font-family": TEXT_FAMILY,
        });
        container.append(svg);
        const layerBase = el("g", {class: "layer-base"}, svg);
        const layerHit = el("g", {class: "layer-hit"}, svg);
        const layerText = el("g", {class: "layer-text"}, svg);

        // —— 最下层：边界圆、宫位线、真实位置刻度
        for (const i of VISIBLE_CIRCLES) {
            el("circle", {cx: C, cy: C, r: R[i], class: `ring ring-${i}`}, layerBase);
        }
        cusps.forEach((lon, i) => {
            const a = screen(lon);
            const [x1, y1] = polar(R[13], a);
            const [x2, y2] = polar(R[5], a);
            const angular = i % 3 === 0;      // 第 1、4、7、10 宫宫头线稍粗
            el("line", {x1, y1, x2, y2, class: `house-line${angular ? " angular" : ""}`}, layerBase);
        });

        // —— 第 12 圈：宫位数字（两条宫头线的正中间）
        cusps.forEach((lon, i) => {
            const a1 = screen(lon);
            const span = norm360(cusps[(i + 1) % 12] - lon);
            const [x, y] = polar(R[12], a1 + span / 2);
            drawText(layerText, x, y, {cls: "house-num", text: String(i + 1), size: FONT.house, family: TEXT_FAMILY, weight: 600});
        });

        // —— 第 2 圈：宫头的星座度数
        const labels = cusps.map((lon, i) => ({angle: screen(lon), parts: cuspLabelParts(lon), house: i + 1, lon}))
            .sort((a, b) => a.angle - b.angle);
        const labelAngles = spreadOnCircle(labels, cuspNeed);
        labels.forEach((label, k) => {
            const c = labelAngles[k];
            const total = cuspLabelLength(label.parts, c);
            const g = el("g", {class: "cusp-label"}, layerText);
            el("title", {}, g).textContent = `第 ${label.house} 宫宫头  ${lonText(label.lon)}`;
            let offset = -total / 2;
            for (const part of label.parts) {
                const e = tangentialExtent(part.box, c);
                const angle = c + (offset + e / 2) / R[2] * 180 / Math.PI;
                const [x, y] = polar(R[2], angle);
                drawText(g, x, y, part);
                offset += e + GAP;
            }
        });

        // —— 盘中元素：计算射线角度
        const els = elements.map((item) => ({...item, angle: screen(item.lon), parts: elementParts(item)}))
            .sort((a, b) => a.angle - b.angle);
        let elAngles;
        let overflow = false;
        try {
            elAngles = spreadOnCircle(els, elementNeed);
        } catch (error) {
            if (!error.overflow) throw error;
            overflow = true;
        }

        // —— 第 4 圈：恒星
        const starItems = stars.map((s) => ({...s, angle: screen(s.lon),
            box: textBox(symText(STAR_GLYPH), FONT.star, SYMBOL_FAMILY)})).sort((a, b) => a.angle - b.angle);
        let starAngles = [];
        try {
            starAngles = spreadOnCircle(starItems, (a, b, aa, ab) => angularNeed(a.box, aa, b.box, ab, R[4]));
        } catch (error) {
            if (!error.overflow) throw error;
            overflow = true;
        }
        const starNodes = new Map();
        const starData = new Map();
        starItems.forEach((s, k) => {
            const g = el("g", {class: "chart-star", "data-id": s.id}, layerText);
            const [x, y] = polar(R[4], starAngles[k]);
            el("circle", {cx: x, cy: y, r: 9, class: "star-hit"}, g);
            drawText(g, x, y, {cls: "star-glyph", text: symText(STAR_GLYPH), size: FONT.star, family: SYMBOL_FAMILY,
                weight: 400, style: fillStyle(s.color)});
            starNodes.set(s.id, g);
            starData.set(s.id, s);
        });

        if (overflow) {
            const message = "排盘错误：盘中元素太多，星盘上一圈排不下。请在「星体配置」里少启用一些小行星、特殊点或 Lot（或少一些有相位的恒星）。";
            const [x, y] = [C, C];
            const t = el("text", {x, y, "text-anchor": "middle", "dominant-baseline": "central", class: "chart-error"}, layerText);
            t.textContent = "排盘错误";
            if (lastErrorResults !== results) {
                lastErrorResults = results;
                options.onError?.(message);
            }
            return {elements: els, overflow: true};
        }

        // —— 盘中元素：真实位置刻度 + 5 个子项
        const nodes = new Map();
        els.forEach((item, k) => {
            const a = elAngles[k];
            const [tx1, ty1] = polar(R[5], item.angle);
            const [tx2, ty2] = polar(R[5] - TICK, item.angle);
            el("line", {x1: tx1, y1: ty1, x2: tx2, y2: ty2, class: `el-tick kind-${item.kind}`}, layerBase);

            const g = el("g", {class: `chart-element kind-${item.kind}`, "data-id": item.id}, layerText);
            el("title", {}, g).textContent = tooltipFor(item, aspects);
            // 可点击区域：盖住这条射线上所有子项的一小段扇形（画在文字下面）。
            const half = Math.max(...item.parts.map((p) => tangentialExtent(p.box, a) / 2 / R[p.ring] * 180 / Math.PI)) + 0.6;
            const hit = el("path", {d: sectorPath(R[10] - 10, R[6] + 14, a - half, a + half), class: "el-hit", "data-id": item.id}, layerHit);
            for (const part of item.parts) {
                const [x, y] = polar(R[part.ring], a);
                drawText(g, x, y, part);
            }
            nodes.set(item.id, {g, hit});
        });

        // —— 点击：高亮选中元素以及与它构成相位的元素和恒星
        function applyHighlight() {
            const related = new Set((aspects.index.get(selection) || []).map((x) => x.other));
            for (const [id, {g, hit}] of nodes) {
                const state = id === selection ? "selected" : related.has(id) ? "aspecting" : "";
                g.classList.toggle("selected", state === "selected");
                g.classList.toggle("aspecting", state === "aspecting");
                hit.classList.toggle("selected", state === "selected");
                hit.classList.toggle("aspecting", state === "aspecting");
                g.classList.toggle("dimmed", Boolean(selection) && !state);
            }
            for (const [id, g] of starNodes) {
                g.classList.toggle("aspecting", related.has(id));
                g.classList.toggle("dimmed", Boolean(selection) && !related.has(id));
            }
        }
        svg.addEventListener("click", (event) => {
            const target = event.target.closest("[data-id]");
            const id = target?.dataset.id;
            // 点恒星：弹出恒星信息框，不改变元素的选中状态。
            if (id && starData.has(id)) {
                event.stopPropagation();
                openStarPopup(container, target, starData.get(id), aspects);
                return;
            }
            closeStarPopup();
            if (id && nodes.has(id)) selection = selection === id ? null : id;
            else selection = null;
            applyHighlight();
        });
        applyHighlight();
        return {elements: els, overflow: false};
    }

    function clearSelection() {
        selection = null;
    }

    window.AstroChart = {
        render,
        clearSelection,
        // 仅供调试 / 测试
        _debug: {spreadOnCircle, splitLon},
    };
})();

/*
 * 圆形星盘的大小：改下面两个数即可（改完刷新网页）。
 *   --chart-max-width   星盘最大宽度。调大，星盘就大；调小，星盘就小。
 *   --chart-vh-offset   用窗口高度减去这个值作为星盘宽度的另一个上限，保证打开页面不用滚动就能看到整张盘。
 *                       调小（例如 120px），星盘可以更接近窗口高度；调大，星盘更矮。
 * 星盘最终宽度 = 下面三者中最小的一个：面板宽度、--chart-max-width、窗口高度 − --chart-vh-offset（但不小于 360px）。
 * 星盘是矢量图，所有文字跟着整体一起缩放，不会变糊，也不需要改别的地方。
 */
:root {
    --chart-max-width: 820px;
    --chart-vh-offset: 140px;
}

.chart-box { position: relative; }

.astro-chart {
    width: min(100%, var(--chart-max-width), max(360px, calc(100vh - var(--chart-vh-offset))));
    height: auto;
    user-select: none;
}
